{"parentUuid":"182e1190-82fc-46f2-b407-71608a51462b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0167pTs8KrSZ3JTe9Sd2RnVj","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Euclid\"\n     4→source: [\"World Anvil\"]\n     5→world_anvil_category: \"\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# Euclid\n     9→\n    10→An Owl Aarakocra Wizard who works closely with the Owl Zodiac, Archimedes. A very old individual who has been around since the creation of the Celestial Realm. He got his start as a fledgling archivist working under Gabriel the Kind and worked his way up the ladder to become Gabriel's Keeper of the Divine Seal.\n    11→He did so under the condition that he would be safe from Fiends and so he was taught a Forbiddence Spell to keep him safe. Prior to the Collapse of the 7 Heavens, Euclid was approached by a younger and curious Reynardine of the Autumn Court, whom he saw a spark within and agreed to let the Fey Prince study magic under him. Together they spent countless years learning magic and researching the Progenitors. A book of note that the two of them had an argument over was the Origin of Genasi, a tome which indicated the power of the Elemental Evils and the emergence of the Genasi who rose from the ashes of the calamity they caused. During this time, Reynardine would spend large amounts of time out on missions to recover artifacts for Euclid, including donating his own pan-flute which he traded Euclid for safe keeping. In thanks, he offered the Aarakocra a refuge among his people once he assumed his rightful place as FeyLorde of the Autumn Court. \"I have entrusted you a power greater than the moon, and in payment for your help guarding it, you will be granted from me a favor and the moon\"\n    12→Euclid's Library on the moon was named Alexandria, though he would not take up residence there for some time, the library was tethered to the aarakocra's ancestral home on an isle of clouds, Laputa Town. When the 7 Heaven's fell, Euclid retreated back to Alexandria and entrusted Archimedes to transfer the Forbiddence Spell over to their new refuge. During the Elemental Wars that followed, Euclid made an agreement with the old Air Genasi General and follower of the fallen Reynardine that he would allow Laputa Town to be anchored to the newly formed Cloud City under the condition that he be allowed to modify the Forbiddence Spell into a contract that could only be undone by The Ragnarok Protocol. The new Forbiddence Spell would now apply to Reynaldine of the Autumn Court, the being who now inhabited the once spirited Fey Prince. That would apply to the entirety of Cloud City which would be allowed to use the Immovable Rod central to Laputa Town as a contingency plan in case the king were to die. This agreement occurred as a coalition between the Storm King Terrell Temptus, Euclid, and\n    13→Leonidas of Summer's Last Light\n    14→. All three having reached a peace treaty at the end of the conflict. The Fire Genasi agreed to hand over the Water Genasi princess, Windhaven's Harvest Festival was created as a tithe to the Spring Court, and Leonidas was granted a sanctum which would preserve Juliannia's last moments forever.\n    15→Recently, the Ragnarok Protocol was activated, removing the Forbiddence Spell from Alexandria, Cloud City and WindHaven. Stalwart, Nyx and Brigit (using her Owl Zodiac Form) were able to re-activate the spell, however Xyn with her patron\n    16→Mr. Happy\n    17→stayed behind to finish charging the spell.\n    18→"}]},"uuid":"95790ec2-e471-4c44-b6dd-2515a1191cb6","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:07.654Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"182e1190-82fc-46f2-b407-71608a51462b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"7dabb8f1-5ec5-452c-ba7a-5d4e84aa80c5","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01YRGV8AgkZtNnbQ6bhuuvUA","toolUseID":"toolu_01YRGV8AgkZtNnbQ6bhuuvUA","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:08.144Z","uuid":"f524b9cc-cebc-44b7-a391-6c0c0eefd162","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"c91af2f4-70bb-40d6-b7a2-57e05ef64a98","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YRGV8AgkZtNnbQ6bhuuvUA","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Gabriel the Kind\"\n     4→source: [\"World Anvil\"]\n     5→world_anvil_category: \"\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# Gabriel the Kind\n     9→\n    10→The patron of Xyn Khamestria. One of the Triumvirate of Archangels and the more often than not, the tiebreaker between the others. His Divine Seal was left with Euclid whom he had gifted the responsibility and dominion over in an effort to spend more time with a young Fey Prince, Reynardine of the Autumn Court.\n    11→His essence is currently split between three places as opposed to the other two Archangels who only exist in a duality. The majority of the entity that makes up Gabriel has been lost within the Abomination that is Reynaldine of the Autumn Court which was created when the Demon Prince Grazz't tricked the starcrossed Juliannia of the Summer Court into falling in love with him and stealing the Divine Seal from Euclid, which is still one of the 13 Seals of\n    12→Asmodeus The Bringer of Oblivion\n    13→. Grazz't had previously made a deal with Tiamat in exchange for power which ultimately lead to the creation of the Divine Seals and the Celestial Realm and used Juliannia's broken heart as a way to manipulate the poor girl. Her betrothed Reynardine, was in love with another, Gabriel, which made the process so much easier for the fiend. Reunited with the lost Seal, Grazz't power stood unrivaled among his kind, and so he claimed dominion over the 9 Hells, the battle ground on which the Blood War is fought. Juliannia was betrayed, and in her grief, Grazz't imprisoned her within his own Demonic Seal in order to steal her dominion over the Fey Wilde as well as the 9 Hells, confident in his ability to crush his rivals. However this act was discovered by Gabriel and Reynardine and both entities believed they were at fault. Their bond had all but given poor Juliannia over to temptation and before Reynardine could think to act, Gabriel sacrificed himself. Grazz't, as a selfish fiend, was unable to fathom the level of self sacrifice that the Archangel was capable of and the Divine Seal was destroyed, along with it the enchantment that originally created the Celestial Realm AND the 9 Hells. Both Grazz't and Gabriel were imprisoned within the broken seal, the price of their meddling with forces beyond their means. However watching this whole exchange was the now completely distraught Reynardine, who now holding the shattered Divine Seal and the Demonic Amulet that was Juliannia, vowed to fix everything. Reynaldine of the Autumn Court was created out of that vow, all four entities now wrapped up into one.\n    14→The second location of Gabriel's Power is within the Harvest's Zenith. A cruel twist of irony, this Zenith is locked behind a door that requires the four seals of the Elemental Evil's to be combined to open the door. It is the charge of all three archangels along with the Raven Queen to lend their power to the shield that prevents any other extra-petal beings from entering Mardov.\n    15→The third location of Gabriel is inside a small stuffed bear which is carried by Xyn whom she has so affectionately named Mr. Happy after the way that people light up upon touching the bear. This is the weakest connection that Gabriel has, however it is currently the most active. Only through the pact made with Xyn is this possible. Gabriel was the first to bind himself to a mortal, however the other two Archangels have learned this trick as well. Only through this small crack in Gabriel's prison may he influence the world and prevent Guard' Daemon.\n    16→"}]},"uuid":"85e63096-35a6-4694-8cbb-bb74c218ed5d","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:08.144Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c91af2f4-70bb-40d6-b7a2-57e05ef64a98","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"747abc56-5282-4e88-bde7-b139763d2f81","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016DWzpDVN2WFczdpSiANBBq","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Master Timeline\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Master Timeline of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## Ancient History\n     9→\n    10→- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] — original twin souls, divine jealousy begins\n    11→- [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] founds Circle of the Murder ([[Mordred|Ramses]]) + [[Artificer's Guild]] ([[Xerxes]])\n    12→- [[Mordred|Ramses]] forces lichdom on [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]; she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. Sewer descent. |\n    47→| 1 | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] pickpockets note from [[Jorgen]] — proof of setup. |\n    48→| 2 | [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]]. \"2,000 souls.\" [[Jorgen]] killed, becomes revenant. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. Soul tether. |\n    50→| 6 | Dopplegangers. [[V-Mardov]] rift opens. [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from alternate reality. |\n    51→| 7-8 | [[Jorgen]] revenant pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]]. [[Thornberry Shade]] ship. [[jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[Mia]]). [[Mia]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Tiefling prejudice arc. |\n    62→| 19 | Soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]. |\n    63→| 23 | Zodiac spirits revealed — they bet their immortality on the champions. |\n    64→| 24-25 | Tomb of the Last Curator. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Nocturnus Wayne]] kills cousin Dusken, becomes Drider King. |\n    69→| 36 | [[Fen Darkeyes|Devin Darkeyes]] found enslaved by the Aboleth. |\n    70→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc. [[forgesworn]] connections revealed. |\n    71→\n    72→### Act 3 (Chapters 41-86) — The Escalation\n    73→\n    74→| Ch. | Event |\n    75→|-----|-------|\n    76→| 42+ | Campaign splits into parallel tracks. |\n    77→| ~66 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]]'s Homunculus Thraalm killed. |\n    78→| ~82 | Attempted revival of [[Zodiac|Falak]] the serpent using captured Mahen. |\n    79→| 86 | Reunion. Battle of the Distillery. |\n    80→\n    81→### Act 4 (Chapters 87-136) — The Endgame\n    82→\n    83→| Ch. | Event |\n    84→|-----|-------|\n    85→| 103 | Second Reunion. TPK and revival via myconid network. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] carrying Kelemtor. |\n    86→| 104-105 | [[Reginald Providence]] killed (kenku in disguise). [[Anchor of War]] silenced. Therzadun thwarted. Silence/Timothy unmasked as Therzadun's vessel. |\n    87→| 106 | Declaration of War on gods. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar created in image of [[Ashara Everstar]]. |\n    89→| 108 | Three races of elves' gods revealed. Casaval reveals [[Irethia]] (prototype), [[Riven Fen|Fen]] (warden), [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] (bane). [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    90→| 109 | [[Josh Coolbreeze]] cleanses [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] of vampirism. [[lolth]]-[[Raven Queen]] deal revealed. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. Hearthstone exposed as triple agent. |\n    95→| 135 | Book of Shadows plot to open Harvest and Winter Zenith. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→\n   151→Inheritors of the Mantle\n   152→\n   153→In the Death of Kolyma, the potential he captured to answer the Curator’s Plea was bestowed in four mantles.\n   154→\n   155→The Alabaster Mantle of Arcane Magic\n   156→\n   157→The Ivy Mantle of Natural Magic\n   158→\n   159→The Palladium Mantle of Divine Magic\n   160→\n   161→The Crystalline Mantle of Mortal Magic\n   162→"}]},"uuid":"64a97982-b9d3-4216-b06e-ea2b19090dfc","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:08.150Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"747abc56-5282-4e88-bde7-b139763d2f81","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"616a32fd-8759-4b59-9812-7a32370b2c44","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D9tXn52tLVdJvdYrdMp8h2","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 38X - Nyx s notes - Euclid s Study\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_38X_-_Nyx_s_notes_-_Euclid_s_Study.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→Notes from Chapter 38X – Euclid’s Study\n     8→\n     9→(These were relayed from Nyx and Stalwart)\n    10→\n    11→Suddenly, Stalwart and Nyx are pulled through a portal!\n    12→\n    13→It's different from our normal portals, more jarring.\n    14→\n    15→They also see visions of an owl. Linda's mimicking, and Hedwig is nowhere to be found\n    16→\n    17→They land!\n    18→\n    19→And are greeted by an owl humanoid creature amongst a pile of books.\n    20→\n    21→Apparently he only wanted one of them.\n    22→\n    23→They're in a huge library, glowing blue somehow.\n    24→\n    25→Behind the owl is a window blinking yellow-white and from there, a huge voice telling Euclid to calm down, and Inchu has asked their help to reclaim the Sunless Citadel - they need to clear out the snakes\n    26→\n    27→Euclid calls this voice Archimedes, and says Nyx (the Tome-Sworn) is on thin ice.\n    28→\n    29→Euclid was the keeper of the Divine Seal, but he is no longer.\n    30→\n    31→His enemy is also Reynaldine of the Autumn Court.\n    32→\n    33→Reynaldine\n    34→\n    35→Apparently his powers are not entirely his own\n    36→\n    37→He was once a Prince of Harvest\n    38→\n    39→Now, he's the Ruler of the Feywilde West of the Glittering Run\n    40→\n    41→And those under the Queen of Thorns call him an Abomination.\n    42→\n    43→Be careful though, we might not want this information - for knowledge is power.\n    44→\n    45→He might not know, but he'll know that Euclid talked to Nyx.\n    46→\n    47→All the Lore!\n    48→\n    49→7 heavens were ruled by 3 entities: Barachiel the Just, Dominel the Righteous and Gabriel the Kind. They were gifted domain of at least one celestial plane.\n    50→\n    51→Prime: Gabriel.\n    52→\n    53→9 Hells - Lords of the Nine and the Princes of the Abyss. And they had a blood war over it and Asmodeus's seals.\n    54→\n    55→Nobody thankfully is stupid enough to release him.\n    56→\n    57→Conflict:\n    58→\n    59→2 fated betrothed in love with beings above them.\n    60→\n    61→Reynaldine and Juliannia Princess of the Summer Solstice\n    62→\n    63→Gabriel the Rind and Graz'zt.\n    64→\n    65→Juliannia was bewitched by Graz'zt, and elevated the Ambitious Devil into a demon, Juliannia becoming his Demon Amulet.\n    66→\n    67→Graz'zt became the Demon Prince the Lord of Pleasure.\n    68→\n    69→Gabriel condensed himself and the Demon Prince into Graz'zt's Demonic Amulet\n    70→\n    71→Reynaldine took the amulet, twisting himself.\n    72→\n    73→He was never forgiven by most, especially the Queen of Thorns, Evelyandrin of the Spring Court, sister of Juliannia.\n    74→\n    75→And yes, it was Reynaldine's fault. He snuck off to meet with Gabriel and caused Juliannia to search for him.\n    76→\n    77→Present time\n    78→\n    79→Reynaldine took Gabriel's Divine Seal and caused the collapse of the Celestial Realm, and further collapsed the 9 Hells.\n    80→\n    81→They aren't gone, but they've merged into the FeyWilde.\n    82→\n    83→Most citizens are held captive in a ruined human city in the feywilde\n    84→\n    85→ALSO His Origins of Genasi book is missing.\n    86→\n    87→Why us?\n    88→\n    89→Not sure.\n    90→\n    91→But! Reynaldine may have 4 pacts within his 4 selves. Nyx's pact may be with Juliannia.\n    92→\n    93→Also, Reynaldine did make the pact with Tygras that began this whole Tiefling nonsense (probably Graz'zt)\n    94→\n    95→Nyx's sister, Xyn, might have a pact with Gabriel\n    96→\n    97→And Reynaldine himself might be saving his pact.\n    98→\n    99→The Queen of Thorns is within the Feywilde, her kingdom almost gone as the Autumn Court's forces have pushed them into the briar patch\n   100→\n   101→Do not mention the theatre, her sister. AND NO RED.\n   102→\n   103→The goblin tribes were lured and sacrificed to the great serpent, but there should be a few outliers about.\n   104→\n   105→Goals!\n   106→\n   107→Euclid believes it possible to bring Juliannia back with Gabriel. But we'll need Xyn and Tygras, and Reynaldine's pactbound.\n   108→"}]},"uuid":"e9b57aac-0ad0-48d1-9087-90a7bb40534d","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:20.638Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"616a32fd-8759-4b59-9812-7a32370b2c44","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"153f2ccc-9cfa-4fac-8d11-4cd8627b8199","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MMDs6tMPDyLEEPeUjra1Qj","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Cloud City Arc\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Cloud City Arc.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→- The party arrived in WindHaven via a portal opened by the **Kraken of the Sand Seas** to one of the Qual'Xar Vessels, **the Flooded Roots**, moored at the port safely tucked away inside the cliff face where the rest of the city rests\n    10→\n    11→- The party headed up the switchbacks up to the city proper and were greeted by **Tiberious D Rex** who had received a premonition that the party would be passing through there\n    12→\n    13→- The party headed to the center of town where the massive Windmill Willow grows, meeting Kelik Temptus, crown prince of the wind genasi, who needed a bit more time to get ready to make the journey through the everstorm surrounding WindHaven\n    14→\n    15→- The party then ventured into the library held within the truck of the tree for some research and shopping\n    16→\n    17→  - Brigit researched the zodiacs\n    18→\n    19→    - grabs the astral chart that\\'s been transcribed over a year.\n    20→\n    21→      - Not all of them depict the 12, but on the first day of each month, there\\'s a different animal.\n    22→\n    23→      - Current Month is the Rabbit, Nivens, and then next is the Pangolin, Osiris!\n    24→\n    25→  - Tesca researches her hometown\n    26→\n    27→    - the History of Dawnpass \\<about the founding of the village\\>\n    28→\n    29→    - the Origin of the Dawn \\<about the creation of the diadem of the dawn\\>\n    30→\n    31→  - Randal researched the gods\n    32→\n    33→    - He also got a recipe for preventing and curing dementia \\<all ingredients must be consumed at the same time\\>\n    34→\n    35→  - Nyx was confronted due to her connection with an entity that she is not familiar\n    36→\n    37→- While the others were doing some research, Sprocket confronted a scroll shopkeep and decided to attempt to mind-control the condescending librarian which caused the entire tree's worth of halflings to descend on the party\n    38→\n    39→- After getting chased out of the tree by a mob of angry \"squirrels\", the party sought refuge with Kelik who created a barrier to house the party, but a large sphinx descended from the top of the willow\n    40→\n    41→- The sphinx teleported the party to his demi-plane housed within the leaves of trees and announced himself as **Leonidas of Summer's Last Light**, the party was then put on trial\n    42→\n    43→  - The trial consisted of 3 challenges which were denoted by the three roars of the sphinx, however only the first trial was meant to determine innocence, the second was to denote worthyness and the third was only completed by Fen Darkeyes who was granted a wish through time\n    44→\n    45→    - Varys requested help in the fight against Vecna\n    46→\n    47→      - He also is granted a vision of the present where he sees **Daina Ironforge** grab a red gem \\<Heart of the Forge\\> and jump into a pool of lava\n    48→\n    49→    - Nyx requested help to save her sister, Xyn\n    50→\n    51→      - She is granted a chat with a blonde woman with warm colored leaves all over her body like clothing. She has a summery voice, this is **Juliannia of the Summer Court**, her real patron\\...\n    52→\n    53→    - Fen asked that he and his brother had never fallen under the influence of the Aboleth who had torn them apart and forced him to kill his brother, this request was granted, though circumstances that led to him joining the party remained, his brother, **Devin BrightHallow** is supposedly still in Monethern City working as the captain of **The Verdant Horizon**\n    54→\n    55→  - Stalwart who had suffered from a serious advancement in age of 14 years requested that for his boon he was returned to his normal age safe and sound... he headed to the Traveler's Pony for a pint\n    56→\n    57→  - Of the worthy Stalwart, Nyx, Varys, Tiberious, and eventually (after completing the second trial) Fen\n    58→\n    59→  - Sprocket alone was left unworthy, though the meaning remains a mystery...\n    60→\n    61→    - He did however later dimension door out of the pocket dimension... and go get a pint at the Traveler's Pony as well\n    62→\n    63→  - Tiberious was the only member to accompany Fen in the third trial, it was revealed that he had undergone this process 75 times ... making him much older than the 76 he appeared to be, he refused to answer more questions\n    64→\n    65→    - Ultimately he succumbed and backed out of the third trial crying for \"Juliania\" (is this the **Juliania of the Summer Court**, and Nyx's Patron?)\n    66→\n    67→- Meanwhile, Randal and Tesca were exploring the Traveler's Pony and uncovered that it was a hideout for the **Cabal of Horns**, an underground society of Tieflings who were led by a scarlet tiefling named: **Winnepeg Redvine**\n    68→\n    69→  - The two were warned of a plot by a secret society who had been kidnapping children and shipping them to Dawnpass, the local lord **Mathia Nuum** was providing the front, her symbol is: A Red Sailed Fishing Vessel\n    70→\n    71→  - There was a pale tiefling who had been recently rescued, however Randal and Tesca were unable to explore **Umbertown**, the name for the tiefling underground, further because the bouncer had been murdered by a mysterious pair of masquerade mask wearing people who killed with poison and fled the brothel\n    72→\n    73→- The party secured the help of a few **Farmer's Daughters**, local farmers turned mercenaries who's mammoths were able to traverse the everstorm surrounding Windhaven, however when they met up with their caravan at the north gate, they uncovered one of Nuum's shipments of fish ... upon further investigation of their goods, they found the latest shipment of children\n    74→\n    75→  - Dispatching the guards who turned into monstrous snake beings, the party entrusted the safety of the few orphaned children (**Reela, Rory and Squat)** that they managed to save to the Farmer's Daughters as they had more pressing matters at Cloud City\n    76→\n    77→- The path to the \"Second Eye\" of the everstorm outside of town was paved with the bodies of numerous orcs and humans who had died in combat, there was a strange thick black ooze which coated the fallen, none of their weapons and armor could be found\n    78→\n    79→  - There was a large red dragon who could be seen above the cloud cover, it seemed to be attacking a caravan in the distance, however the party attempted to keep a low profile.\n    80→\n    81→- After they looked to have been spotted by the dragon, the party was \"rescued\" after stumbling into a tranquil circle of autumn trees, in its center on a tree stump, stood **Reynaldine of the Autumn Court**, a creature whose true origin was recently uncovered by Nyx to be an abomination of 4 entities with deep regret for his own existence.\n    82→\n    83→  - He told the party that he had been collecting the Elemental Seals to the Harvest's Zenith and that he intended to \"Fix Everything\" with them, Nyx who's connection with this entity goes deeper than purely the power he supplies as part of his patronage decided to help him... it was that or deal with the clearly overmatched FeyLord and the Dracolich who gave him a lift here: **Rimefang**\n    84→\n    85→  - **Kelik Temptus, Prince of the Air Genasi and Keeper of the Seal of Temptus** died that day when he was absorbed back into his amulet upon it being handed over to Reynaldine\n    86→\n    87→  - Claiming to have already collected the Fire and Earth seals, Reynaldine tasked the party with retrieving the water amulet which was on **Lapis Poseidon**, the water genasi princess who hung in a glass prison within Kelik's Father's throne-room\n    88→\n    89→  - During the fight, Brigit discovered that her stag form resembled the real Stag Zodiac, **Shi Shi Gami**, who's antlers formed the locket that is displayed across her forehead\n    90→\n    91→- The party was then left in the Everstorm without their guides still tasked with heading to Cloud City, however this time to retrieve the last seal to the Harvest's Zenith\n    92→\n    93→  - Thanks to Brigit's newfound Air Elemental Power, the party was able to fly above the storm and make it to the Second Eye, but were attacked by some Giants and their pet Roc's\n    94→\n    95→  - The party fled through a lightning struck birch tree after activating an ancient network of trees which seem to interlock every major city in Mardov\n    96→\n    97→- The party made their way to **The Roost Hotel and Casino** where they were greeted by the Penguin Aarakocra, **Mr. Tux**, who informed the party of the fiends who are assaulting **Euclid's Study** within the **Guest Services** portion of the hotel\n    98→\n    99→  - Brigit, Fen and Varys went directly to the **Temple of the 9 Winds** where the throneroom of **Terrell Temptus**, **the Storm King**. They learned that he had already discovered that there was a plot to kidnap the water genasi princess and they were tasked with rooting out the culprit and bringing them to the king, they too headed back to join the others at the Roost with a royal letter, granting them the Royal Suite at the top of the hotel... for free!\n   100→\n   101→  - The others headed to the **Ice-House** to exchange their gold for the local form of currency known as **Electrum**, there they met a Bronze Dragon named **Zepheeron** who took a liking to Sprocket's \"generocity\"\n   102→\n   103→    - They learned the **Zepheeron** is the guardian of a strangely shaped building known as **The Hanging Gardens** however he also lends his lightning to the device which creates Electrum two gold pieces are fused with the lightning and a combination of Cirrus and Nimbus bricks\n   104→\n   105→    - The party passed the **Hanging Gardens** in **Nimbus Town**, the temple is noteworthy as it is in the shape of two pyramids stacked at their square bases, the temple looks to be in three parts, a center and two pyramid tops and bottoms. The striped pattern has lavish vines and other foliage that drape its sides partially filling in the gaps left in the building.\n   106→\n   107→- Back at the Roost, Randal was playing for free room and board (not knowing that the others already secured it) when he was surrounded by agents of **Lorelai Clary** who proceeded to sabotage his performance and capture him, they were dodged thanks to his friends coming to the rescue but **Fen Darkeyes** was taken in his stead by one of the two twin Succubus/Incubus named **Minnie LaDoux**\n   108→\n   109→  - He was taken down to Guest Services and was given a command: \"Head to the Throneroom when the Jack Falls and Retrieve the Water Genasi Princess\"\n   110→\n   111→  - The agents of Lorelai's crew turned out to be Hewie, Dewie, and Louie of the travelling siegeball team. Additionally there was a Crane Aarakocra who has yet to be unmasked... (the elder oobliex)\n   112→\n   113→- The Party captured Minnie's brother **Goadie LaDoux**, manager of the Casino when they found him eavesdropping on their room, they impersonated him to get close to Lorelai within the Casino however their meeting turned violent fast:\n   114→\n   115→  - The fighting was over quick when it became apparent that the party was much more experienced in combat than their opponents\n   116→\n   117→  - During the fight, the party had them on the ropes and nearly killed Lorelai\n   118→\n   119→  - Things turned sour when the mother of all distractions occurred .... Jack Fell!\n   120→\n   121→  - Chaos ensued, most of the Red Skipper were handed over to the authories, but the Crane just barely slipped through the grasp of the party\n   122→\n   123→  - Fen had a compulsion activated on him which caused him to also slip away from the others in the following confusion\n   124→\n   125→- In the aftermath of the party:\n   126→\n   127→  - Randal Legend-Lord **Reynaldine of the Autumn Court** and learned that:\n   128→\n   129→    - he is actually 4 beings in one, who we now know the names of:\n   130→\n   131→      - **Juliannia of the Summer Court,** Archfey and Nyx's Patron\n   132→\n   133→      - **Grazz't, The Demon Prince of Pleasure**\n   134→\n   135→      - **Gabriel the Kind,** Archangel\n   136→\n   137→      - **Reynardine. Prince of Harvest**, Archfey who is currently housing the other entities within him\n   138→\n   139→    - The Primal Elemental Evils were Sealed by the 4 archFey before the incident which consumed Juliannia, from the seals the genasi leaked out\n   140→\n   141→    - Tiamat was broken into 5 pieces by Bahamut who was being followed by the Archangels, Gabriel included.... These 3 angels were gifted a seal of their own. The purpose of these seals is unknown\n   142→\n   143→  - Nyx and Stalwart interrogated Lorelai who ended up touching her broach \\<which is magic in its own right\\> and transfer the effects of a dream spell which allowed the two long lost sisters to finally communicate! Nyx finally got to meet **Xyn Khamestria**, her younger sister who now looks the same age\n   144→\n   145→    - Stalwart during this time was seen single handedly wrestling a the owlbear, **Princess**, down to the ground to calm it down during the drone of the mixed gusts of wind that now thrashed about all of Cloud City\n   146→\n   147→    - Nyx learned that:\n   148→\n   149→      - Xyn was aged forward thanks to her interaction with Leonidas earlier in her own story, it is uncertain what caused this event.\n   150→\n   151→      - She believes the key to opening the Harvest's Zenith is written within a book which was originally stolen from **Euclid** by Reynaldine: **The Origin of Genasi *: and The Rebirth of the Progenitors***\n   152→\n   153→      - Xyn has **Gabriel the Kind** as her patron, he lives in the teddy bear that she calls **Mr. Happy**. Making her the second Khamestria to be a patron of the 4 part entity known as Reyanldine\n   154→\n   155→      - The Mountain's Zenith was opened and that let out **Barachiel the Righteous** who had a portion of his power locked within, sparking a partial rebirth within the Shadowfell.\n   156→\n   157→      - Mr. Happy is a small vessel for Gabriel's power, Xyn believes if she can make it to the Shadowfell, that vessel's potential will grow but she knows that if the Havest's Zenith is opened that Gabriel will also be reborn in part.\n   158→\n   159→      - Gabriel is needed in the Shadowfell to quell the fighting between his brothers. \"Without Kindness, there is no way to align Justice and Righteousness\"\n   160→\n   161→      - Xyn is in Cloud City to get ahold of the water amulet so that Reynaldine will open the Havest's Zenith, though Xyn vows to try and lock him out with Gabriel's help\n   162→\n   163→      - Shes not alone, there are multiple factions trying to get ahold of the entity\n   164→\n   165→        - Fire Genasi are angry and headed towards that area\n   166→\n   167→        - Serpents within the water's apparatus are trying to revive an ancient serpent to eat the world\n   168→\n   169→      - Euclid's Study is being assaulted and will not hold for long, they are looking for the Origin of Genasi book.... Of which Xyn has the only copy\n   170→\n   171→  - Fen had a longer adventure where he ended up being framed for the murder of King Temptus and the kidnapping of Lapis, however to escape a truely unfortunate fate he envoked Reynaldine's power, blacking out and waking up on the way back from Guest Services.. Empty handed\n   172→\n   173→- The others re-gathered their party and attempted to stop Cloud City from plummeting into the city below it...\n   174→\n   175→- Tesca leads the reunited party through an urban sprawl to the **Elamier's Flask**, a potion shop in Nimbus town which has a special potion that can replicate any item it touches, however it comes at a great cost. Lorelai had a jar of it set aside for herself and was happy to offer her help in exchange for more ... amenable company\n   176→\n   177→  - Their plan is to use it to repair Jack by lathering its center in the potion\n   178→\n   179→- The party bumps into the bronze dragon that Fen recognizes from his solo adventure... behind enemy lines. Sprocket and Stalwart recognize the dragon from their trip to the bank ... after all the dragon ended up being the one to mint the electrum when surrounded by 12 air genasi all channeling their power\n   180→\n   181→  - **Zepheeron the Bronze** tells the party that he will head to the Apparatus within the Hanging Gardens to help keep Cloud City afloat in the absence of The Storm King or Seal of Temptus...\n   182→\n   183→  - This is the same apparatus that the Storm King referenced, allowed him to scry on Kelik and drag the party into this whole mess\n   184→\n   185→- The party finds 3 factions fighting at Jackfall Landing: The Air Genasi, Attacking Orcs and The Giants who seemingly betrayed their former comrades, the party fought back wave after wave of orcs and after a long seemingly unending fight were finally rescued by **The Thornberry Wilde**, a sky whale ship helmed by **Captain Liza** with her rag-tag crew\n   186→\n   187→  - Notably the orcs are wearing a sigil which denotes Faeish, a village that Brigit grew up near\n   188→\n   189→- Once aboard they witnessed a red dragon and fire genasi on his back attacking the Hanging Garden, specifically a metallic object which is now orbiting the temple.\n   190→\n   191→  - Over the course of the fight, they work out that this is **Blaine Cowen,** Fire Genasi Royal Guard and **Volcanis, the Red**, they are wrestling power away from the Bronze Dragon, they were originally searching for the fire genasi princess, **Ember Pyra** but she was taken by fiends. He head here to scry for her and found this\n   192→\n   193→  - Sprocket touches the satellite and discovers that its currently scrying on a hanging city ... upside-down hanging city with a dual spheroid apparatus on the particular platform within that city that the vision scoped in on behind the platform is a similarly dual pyramid shape with 7 floors all rotating out of phase with each other horizontally ... the Harvest's Zenith!\n   194→\n   195→  - The fight ends and the two sides speak, specifically Blaine comments on the familial connection between **Xyn and Nyx**\n   196→\n   197→"}]},"uuid":"d3f0c460-e85e-42f7-8b0b-14a99a409f26","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:21.078Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"153f2ccc-9cfa-4fac-8d11-4cd8627b8199","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"28882873-4a24-462c-9acb-ae60aee6b5e2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01SDNfPVvi1Sqa7Jb3UPyN3f","toolUseID":"toolu_01SDNfPVvi1Sqa7Jb3UPyN3f","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:22.406Z","uuid":"736cb049-01e7-4e02-a317-07b5c83c009d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"0df9fd1e-c51c-491d-900f-d66a0a8379df","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SDNfPVvi1Sqa7Jb3UPyN3f","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Nocturnus Wayne\"\n     5→aliases: [\"The Loner\", \"The Exile\", \"Darkest Knight\"]\n     6→race: \"Drow\"\n     7→class: \"Monk / Vampire\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive (undead)\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Summary 23\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Nocturnus/Nocturnus Wayne -- Post Vampire.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Nocturnus Wayne is the party's dark knight -- a drow monk turned vampire who kills gods because someone has to, and because the darkness in him is already paid for. He operates alone by preference and in the dark by necessity. His quest to destroy his aunt [[Oblivion Wayne]], the drow vampire queen, expanded into a cosmic role as godslayer and shadow champion. He is the party member who scares everyone and makes everyone laugh, often simultaneously.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **tall, lean male** with **deathly pale grey-white skin** that reads as corpselike in any lighting. His **long white-platinum hair** falls past his shoulders in lank, straight strands, framing a face with **dark, sunken predatory eyes** set deep in shadowed sockets. His features are gaunt, angular, and aristocratic in the way that death is aristocratic.\n    23→\n    24→He wears **black spiked armor** covered in organic, thorn-like blade protrusions -- the armor looks less forged and more grown, as though darkness itself crystallized into protective plate. Dark leather and metal layers are secured with multiple **buckles and straps**, creating a silhouette of controlled menace. Behind his shoulders, **wing-like blade protrusions** extend upward -- not wings he can fly with, but decorative barbs of dark metal that echo a bat's wingspan.\n    25→\n    26→In his right hand he grips a **greatsword** -- his companion weapon, Bane -- with casual, intimate familiarity. His left hand drips with **fresh blood**, the only warm color in his entire palette. The blood catches light that his skin does not.\n    27→\n    28→The color palette is intentional: cool blue-grey, black armor, white hair, and the singular shock of red blood. Every element reads gothic, vampiric, and dangerous. He is the visual embodiment of the party's darkest capabilities.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**INTJ (The Architect)** -- Strategic, brooding, relentlessly goal-oriented. He has the emotional range of a gothic cathedral: vast, echoing, mostly in shadow, with occasional shafts of light that are more striking for their rarity.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Fulfill the prophecy -- kill the gods that need killing. Master the vampire within. Secretly, he wants to be human again. Not the power, not the immortality -- just the warmth.\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Becomes the thing that monsters are afraid of. His pale skin goes translucent, his movements blur, his blade drinks blood in a \"dusty, molasses pull.\" He fights with Bane in a partnership more intimate than any living relationship. Uses Empty Body to become untouchable.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Follows a code that exists entirely in his own head. Chose not to win when winning would cost more than losing. Will never turn another person. Will never feed on the unwilling.\n    39→\n    40→His Summary 23 worldview: \"It becomes evident that I'll have to save these fools. Again.\"\n    41→\n    42→## Backstory\n    43→\n    44→Nocturnus is a drow nobleman whose aunt, [[Oblivion Wayne]], is the leader of the Drow and a vampire queen. She married [[Ebon Wayne]], a vampire drow who captured the [[Bat Zodiac]] and uses it to create alpha vampires. Oblivion turned her own kin to create an army of loyal vampires, using each turned individual's innate loyalty to depose her brother and unite the drow.\n    45→\n    46→Nocturnus was turned against his will. He carries the vampirism as both curse and weapon, seeking vengeance against his aunt while struggling with the hunger inside him. His acquisition of the sentient evil sword [[Nightmare]] nearly consumed him -- the party had to physically restrain him (Brigit tried to polymorph him into a bunny, Sylvia stunned him) before he could be fitted with a \"pacifying spore\" from the mushroom Sovereign covering half his face.\n    47→\n    48→## Key Moments\n    49→\n    50→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    51→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    52→| 0 | Original intake; appears from nowhere in combat | Established as the one who is already there when you notice him |\n    53→| 8 | Party encounters drow forces | His heritage becomes relevant; drow warlock kills Nyx's owl |\n    54→| 23 | Summary: \"I'll have to save these fools. Again.\" | Defines his self-image as reluctant protector |\n    55→| 27 | Uses Slow Fall to save [[Daina Fireforge]] | Despite his \"I save these fools\" attitude, he repeatedly protects others |\n    56→| 36 | Defeats cousin Duskin; absorbs Nightmare sword fragment | The corruption deepens |\n    57→| 38 | Kills hags the party was trying to save | \"Our resident evildoer decides to kill the evil hags. We tried to stop him.\" Party restrains him. |\n    58→| 100+ | Pacifying spore covers half his face | Visible metaphor: literally half himself, half something darker |\n    59→| 136 | Absorbs fragment of Asmodeus's essence | \"I FEEL LIKE A GOD.\" Crystalline heart, power of a dead god. Party: \"Nocturnous, you good bro?\" |\n    60→\n    61→## Relationships\n    62→\n    63→- **[[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]** -- Two loners who respect competence but disagree on methods. Light paladin vs. dark knight. The Nightmare Sword arc forced confrontation.\n    64→- **[[Varis Laindon]]** -- Unspoken bond. Varis brought Nocturnus back from death with a diamond. They do not discuss it. They do not need to.\n    65→- **[[Daina Fireforge]]** -- Combat partners. She grabs him and screams \"slow fall!\" and rides him down cliffs.\n    66→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- She fights for his soul. Greater Restoration daily. Tried to polymorph him into a bunny. This is love expressed as persistent magical intervention.\n    67→- **Bane** -- His sentient greatsword. Animate the sword as a partner, not a tool. Blood flows UP the blade. When he talks to Bane, his hand relaxes on the hilt like holding hands.\n    68→- **[[Oblivion Wayne]]** -- His aunt. Drow vampire queen. His prophesied target.\n    69→\n    70→## Fighting Style\n    71→\n    72→**Class**: Monk (Way of Shadow) / Vampire abilities.\n    73→\n    74→**Weapons**: Greatsword (Bane); unarmed strikes; the darkness itself.\n    75→\n    76→**Signature abilities**:\n    77→- **Empty Body**: Becomes translucent, untouchable. In this form he looks young -- like the man he was before the turning.\n    78→- **Slow Fall**: Saves allies from fall damage (notably Daina off cliffs).\n    79→- **Vampiric Drain**: Blood flows in \"dusty, molasses pull\" toward his blade.\n    80→- **Shadow Step**: He does not walk into rooms. He is already there when you notice him.\n    81→- **Pocket Sand**: A legitimate tactical option he uses to sneak into people's rooms and throw at them.\n    82→\n    83→**Combat philosophy**: Inhuman fluidity. He does not walk, he glides. He does not turn, he pivots. His hair moves half a second behind his head, like underwater. His blade is an extension of his arm. He fights with Bane as a partner -- the most dangerous two-person unit in the party, except one of them is a sword.\n    84→\n    85→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    86→\n    87→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    88→\n    89→- **Race/Class**: Edgelord vampire aesthetic\n    90→- **Player**: Ben\n    91→- **Key Features**: Kingdom Hearts Nobody / Hot Topic circa MCR outfit; chains and zippers everywhere; doesn't wear traditional armor; scarring\n    92→- **Eyes**: Jade green on the side holding the sword, blood red in the other\n    93→- **Equipment**:\n    94→  - Sword devoid of light and shine (\"almost the way that suede doesn't reflect light\")\n    95→  - Scabbard with jade inlays; sword formerly named \"Envy\"\n    96→- **Art direction**: \"I wanna embarrass him in the most lovingly badass way\"; \"DLC for Kingdom Hearts\"\n    97→- **Audrey's note**: \"Ah yes, the edgelord\"\n    98→\n    99→## Gallery\n   100→\n   101→![[assets/pc-art-source/Nocturnus/Nocturnus Wayne -- Post Vampire.jpg]]\n   102→"}]},"uuid":"ecb91168-5536-44c9-aef4-9c382622d0c9","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:22.406Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0df9fd1e-c51c-491d-900f-d66a0a8379df","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"f8e832e1-520f-404d-a738-b6f702f059b2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KN3nFPC5c4yBoJe22bpvjT","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: handout\n     3→title: \"Nocturnus Prophecy\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Handouts/Nocturnus Prophecy.docx\"]\n     5→given_to: []\n     6→chapter: \"\"\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→1)  Travel to the Vag Mahar\n    10→\n    11→2)  Enter the Mountain's Zenith\n    12→\n    13→3)  Find the Kiln of the First Flame and Light the First Forge\n    14→\n    15→    a)  Will cause an invasion by his aunt, Oblivion Wayne\n    16→\n    17→    b)  Needs to use the warforged to beat back invasion\n    18→\n    19→4)  Seek out Aunt in childhood home, only then can you truely kill her\n    20→\n    21→"}]},"uuid":"3479e3ea-c9b5-4afd-9456-66a300cbcc64","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:23.486Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f8e832e1-520f-404d-a738-b6f702f059b2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"6bd4dd7d-8845-45c2-9f8c-669961908648","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01PrTEkwj6RBiN5JZw5Sixcn","toolUseID":"toolu_01PrTEkwj6RBiN5JZw5Sixcn","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:23.746Z","uuid":"9412aff0-54a3-4100-b6c1-65ffcda41b66","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"9412aff0-54a3-4100-b6c1-65ffcda41b66","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01PrTEkwj6RBiN5JZw5Sixcn","toolUseID":"toolu_01PrTEkwj6RBiN5JZw5Sixcn","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:23.746Z","uuid":"98206103-0444-45b1-b121-11cf2786b73d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"6bd4dd7d-8845-45c2-9f8c-669961908648","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PrTEkwj6RBiN5JZw5Sixcn","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Vecna Sworn\"\n     4→aliases: [\"Vecna's Four Zeniths\", \"The Zenith Operations\"]\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"[[Vecna]]\"\n     7→headquarters: \"[[V-Mardov]]\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Vecna Sworn\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→[[Vecna]]'s conquest of [[Mardov]] is organized through four domains of power — the **Four Zeniths**. Each Zenith has a lieutenant, a target, and a nested web of agents. [[Vecna]] rules [[V-Mardov]] (a parallel dimension mirroring the material plane) and commands all four operations.\n    17→\n    18→**Ultimate Goal:** Destroy all keystones, enable [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion of the material plane.\n    19→\n    20→## The Four Zenith Operations\n    21→\n    22→### Zenith of Death (Forest's Zenith — Machu)\n    23→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Wilde]] (Emerald)\n    24→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Death]] (obsidian sword)\n    25→- **Eye:** Green Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    26→- **Primary Agent:** [[Mordred]] / [[Mordred|Ramses]]\n    27→  - [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] (soul-harvesting front)\n    28→  - [[Jorgen]] (lure, turned revenant)\n    29→  - [[Golden Reach]] demons ([[mandu|Markus]]/Danelle, incubus/succubus in Argos)\n    30→  - Hag Coven (Margaret, Gertrude, Tabitha — brew Pestilence from children's souls)\n    31→  - [[mandu]] (corrupt Argos guard captain)\n    32→  - Reva (Machu Royal Advisor)\n    33→  - Tatiana (the Imposter of Machu)\n    34→  - [[jafar]] (Sally's uncle, hired the Dons, placed [[Nathan Yanagan]] as spy)\n    35→  - [[Costa Una]] (traitor within STB)\n    36→  - Salina (Death Knight Captain), Lord Soth (Death Knight of Vodram)\n    37→- **Secondary Agent:** [[Ned'Zailok]] (Lich, Keeper of Death's Anchor in [[V-Mardov]])\n    38→  - Betrayed Casaval Dekun (Eladrin warrior)\n    39→- **Boss:** Forest Guardian / Forest Titan — awakened by [[Mordred]] using the [[Heart of the Wilde]]. Defeating it transports party to [[V-Mardov]].\n    40→\n    41→### Zenith of War (Mountain's Zenith — Vag'Mahar / Underdark)\n    42→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Forge]] (Garnet)\n    43→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of War]] (hammer-axe-javelin)\n    44→- **Eye:** Red Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    45→- **Primary Agent:** [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] (Illithilich — THE Central Villain)\n    46→  - Created the [[Spell Plague]] (with [[Alhoon Ren]]'s research)\n    47→  - Captured the [[Zodiac|Stag Zodiac]] to locate [[Mountain's Zenith]]\n    48→  - Holds [[Thorkon Fireforge]] captive; plans Ceremorphosis\n    49→  - Beholder relay network for psychic communication\n    50→  - Homunculus Thraalm (killed Ch. 66)\n    51→  - **Research Arm:** [[Alhoon Ren]] (double agent, secretly taking notes) + [[Alhoon Pollivar]] (Umber Hulk facility)\n    52→- **Allied Agent:** [[Oblivion Wayne]] (Vampire Queen of the Drow)\n    53→  - [[Ebon Wayne]] (husband, discovered [[Spell Plague]], captured Bat Zodiac)\n    54→  - Vampire-converted Drow army\n    55→  - Plans overland invasion\n    56→- **Allied Agent:** [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] (Puppetmaster of [[Vag'Mahar]])\n    57→  - Convinced [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] to betray [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    58→  - Then betrayed the deal; hunted all [[forgesworn]]\n    59→  - Missing by late campaign\n    60→- **Nested Conspiracy:** The Dwarven Civil War\n    61→  - Three Thanes: Blackbeard, Brownbeard, Redbeard\n    62→  - Three secret police: Blackguard, Truth's Light, Silent Flame\n    63→  - **ALL THREE secretly run by A SINGLE MIND FLAYER**\n    64→  - Goal: use [[Anchor of War]] to breed better Duergar\n    65→\n    66→### Zenith of Pestilence (Winter's Zenith — Bynar)\n    67→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Faith]] (Sapphire)\n    68→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Pestilence]] (pan flute / Horn of Winter)\n    69→- **Eye:** Blue Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    70→- **Primary Agent:** [[rimefang]] (Undead Dragon)\n    71→  - Raised by [[Vecna]], given a white dragon body via [[Black Hand Clan]] rebirth ritual\n    72→  - Ordered to \"open the [[Winter's Zenith]] and finish the Spellplague\"\n    73→  - Receives pestilence supply shipments\n    74→  - Made one last ride as sacrifice (Ch. 113)\n    75→- **Allied Agent:** Yuan-Ti / [[Scales of Falak]]\n    76→  - Stole the [[Anchor of Pestilence]] from the [[Sunless Citadel]]\n    77→  - Ruined the [[Winter's Curators]] (dragon-kin tribe)\n    78→  - Leaders: Umangi Shiva, Casstantinople Pserephonie, Lady Sessura Vaught, Mathia Nuum, Bree Jasnar\n    79→- **Guardian:** [[Frostragan]] (White Dragon, stole [[Heart of the Faith]])\n    80→- **Tiamat Connection:** [[Oblivion Wayne]] gave her soul to [[Tiamat]]. [[Tiamat]]'s avatar awakened late campaign.\n    81→\n    82→### Zenith of Famine (Harvest's Zenith — Tygras Logath / DawnHaven)\n    83→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Progenitors]] (Ruby)\n    84→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Famine]] ([[Diadem of Dawn]])\n    85→- **Eye:** Orange Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    86→- **Primary Agent:** [[Reynaldine]] (Fey Lord of the Autumn Court) — **TRIPLE AGENT**\n    87→  - Serves [[Vecna]] but has his OWN agenda\n    88→  - Wants [[DawnHaven]] cleansed of [[uma]]'s remnants to find the [[Diadem of Dawn|Diadem]]\n    89→  - Hired Annie Hallowbrook to bring [[Kelik Waystar]] to [[windhaven]]\n    90→  - Used [[Xyn]] as messenger\n    91→  - **True Plan (Ch. 133):** Orchestrated bringing ALL villains together at the Convergence + Stone Table so he could ascend to godhood and save Gabriel\n    92→  - Invoked the Ritual of the Stone Table through Umbara Skuttle\n    93→- **Allied Agent:** [[Logath HellEye]] (Emperor of [[Tygras Logath]])\n    94→  - Former gladiator pit champion\n    95→  - Made deal with same Fae demon as [[Tygras]]\n    96→  - Causes bloodlust and madness among orcs\n    97→- **The Orc-Human War** — manufactured by [[Reynaldine]]/[[Logath HellEye|Logath]]/[[Mordred]]\n    98→  - [[Gordan BlackHand]] (Orc Sorcerer, Vecna agent)\n    99→  - [[Grimnak Norman|Grim'Nak the Terrible]], Sledge (Orc Chieftains)\n   100→- **Sealed Evil:** [[Tygras]] — Ancient tiefling warlord sealed inside the Zenith, feeding off its energy\n   101→\n   102→## Allies & Enemies\n   103→\n   104→### Allies\n   105→- [[Therzadun Sworn]] (entangled but separate)\n   106→- [[Graz'zt]] (Demon Prince, held [[Heart of the Progenitors]])\n   107→- [[Asmodeus]] (sealed with 10/13 seals — killed Ch. 136)\n   108→\n   109→### Enemies\n   110→- The Awakened (the party)\n   111→- [[Artificer's Guild]]\n   112→- [[Conquis Abbey]]\n   113→- The Zodiac spirits\n   114→- [[Silver Scales of Bahamut]]\n   115→"}]},"uuid":"49f3790c-945b-47b8-929e-f47c90a3e7a4","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:23.746Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6bd4dd7d-8845-45c2-9f8c-669961908648","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"6093a177-3a84-490d-8dfe-17d0cb17f2d3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Whw2KB9SvchVzPP5JUMn7U","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: creature\n     3→name: \"Oblivion Wayne Ascended\"\n     4→source: [\"D&D Beyond Homebrew\"]\n     5→ac: 17\n     6→hp: 400\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→# Oblivion Wayne Ascended\n    10→\n    11→**AC** 17 | **HP** 400\n    12→**Stats**: STR 18 | DEX 18 | CON 18 | INT 17 | WIS 21 | CHA 22\n    13→**Senses**: Darkvision 120 ft.\n    14→**Skills**: Stealth +10, Religion +9, Insight +11, Perception +11\n    15→**Condition Immunities**: Charmed, Frightened, Paralyzed, Poisoned\n    16→\n    17→## Special Traits\n    18→\n    19→Fey Ancestry. The drow has advantage on saving throws against being charmed, and magic can&rsquo;t put the drow to sleep.\n    20→Innate Spellcasting. The drow&rsquo;s innate spellcasting ability is Charisma (spell save DC 20). She can innately cast the following spells, requiring no material components:\n    21→At will: dancing lights, detect magic\n    22→1/day each: clairvoyance, darkness, detect thoughts, dispel magic, faerie fire, levitate (self only), suggestion\n    23→Lolth&rsquo;s Fickle Favor. As a bonus action, the matron can bestow the Spider Queen&rsquo;s blessing on one ally she can see within 30 feet of her. The ally takes 7 (2d6) psychic damage but has advantage on the next attack roll it makes until the end of its next turn.\n    24→Magic Resistance. The drow has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.\n    25→Spellcasting. The drow is a 20th-level spellcaster. Her spellcasting ability is Wisdom (spell save DC 19, +11 to hit with spell attacks). The drow has the following cleric spells prepared:\n    26→Cantrips (at will): guidance, mending, resistance, sacred flame, thaumaturgy\n    27→1st level (4 slots): bane, command, cure wounds, guiding bolt\n    28→2nd level (3 slots): hold person, silence, spiritual weapon\n    29→3rd level (3 slots): bestow curse, clairvoyance, dispel magic, spirit guardians\n    30→4th level (3 slots): banishment, death ward, freedom of movement, guardian of faith\n    31→5th level (3 slots): contagion, flame strike, geas, mass cure wounds\n    32→6th level (2 slots): blade barrier, harm\n    33→7th level (2 slots): divine word, plane shift\n    34→8th level (1 slot): holy aura\n    35→9th level (1 slot): gate\n    36→Sunlight Sensitivity. While in sunlight, the drow has disadvantage on attack rolls, as well as on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.\n    37→Shapechanger.&nbsp;If the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it can use its action to polymorph into a Tiny&nbsp;bat&nbsp;or a Medium cloud of mist, or back into its true form.\n    38→While in&nbsp;bat&nbsp;form, the vampire can't speak, its walking speed is 5 feet, and it has a flying speed of 30 feet. Its statistics, other than its size and speed, are unchanged. Anything it is wearing transforms with it, but nothing it is carrying does. It reverts to its true form if it dies.\n    39→While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless, has a flying speed of 20 feet, can hover, and can enter a hostile creature's space and stop there. In addition, if air can pass through a space, the mist can do so without squeezing, and it can&rsquo;t pass through water. It has advantage on Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution saving throws, and it is immune to all nonmagical damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight\n    40→Misty Escape.&nbsp;When it drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire transforms into a cloud of mist (as in the Shapechanger trait) instead of falling&nbsp;unconscious, provided that it isn't in sunlight or running water. If it can't transform, it is destroyed.\n    41→While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't revert to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it reverts to its vampire form. It is then&nbsp;paralyzed&nbsp;until it regains at least 1 hit point. After spending 1 hour in its resting place with 0 hit points, it regains 1 hit point.\n    42→Regeneration.&nbsp;The vampire regains 20 hit points at the start of its turn if it has at least 1 hit point and isn't in sunlight or running water. If the vampire takes radiant damage or damage from holy water, this trait doesn't function at the start of the vampire's next turn.\n    43→Spider Climb.&nbsp;The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including upside down on ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.\n    44→Vampire Weaknesses.&nbsp;The vampire has the following flaws:\n    45→Forbiddance.&nbsp;The vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from one of the occupants.\n    46→Harmed by Running Water.&nbsp;The vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.\n    47→Stake to the Heart.&nbsp;If a piercing weapon made of wood is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is&nbsp;incapacitated&nbsp;in its resting place, the vampire is&nbsp;paralyzed&nbsp;until the stake is removed.Sunlight Hypersensitivity.&nbsp;The vampire takes 20 radiant damage when it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.\n    48→\n    49→## Actions\n    50→\n    51→Multiattack.&nbsp;The matron mother makes two demon staff attacks or three tentacle rod attacks or two bite attacks.&nbsp;\n    52→Demon Staff.&nbsp;Melee Weapon Attack:&nbsp;+10 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target.&nbsp;Hit:&nbsp;7 (1d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage, or 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage if used with two hands, plus 14 (4d6) psychic damage. In addition, the target must succeed on a DC 19 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened of the drow for 1 minute. The frightened target can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.\n    53→Tentacle Rod.&nbsp;Melee Weapon Attack:&nbsp;+9 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target.&nbsp;Hit:&nbsp;3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage. If the target is hit three times by the rod on one turn, the target must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution saving throw or suffer the following effects for 1 minute: the target&rsquo;s speed is halved, it has disadvantage on Dexterity saving throws, and it can&rsquo;t use reactions. Moreover, on each of its turns, it can take either an action or a bonus action, but not both. At the end of each of its turns, it can repeat the saving throw, ending the effect on itself on a success.\n    54→Summon Servant (1/Day).&nbsp;The drow magically summons a retriever or a yochlol. The summoned creature appears in an unoccupied space within 60 feet of its summoner, acts as an ally of its summoner, and can&rsquo;t summon other demons. It remains for 10 minutes, until it or its summoner dies, or until its summoner dismisses it as an action.\n    55→Charm.&nbsp;The vampire targets one humanoid it can see within 30 feet of it. If the target can see the vampire, the target must succeed on a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw against this magic or be&nbsp;charmed&nbsp;by the vampire. The&nbsp;charmed&nbsp;target regards the vampire as a trusted friend to be heeded and protected. Although the target isn't under the vampire's control, it takes the vampire's requests or actions in the most favorable way it can, and it is a willing target for the vampire's bite attack.\n    56→Each time the vampire or the vampire's companions do anything harmful to the target, it can repeat the saving throw, ending the effect on itself on a success. Otherwise, the effect lasts 24 hours or until the vampire is destroyed, is on a different plane of existence than the target, or takes a bonus action to end the effect.Bite. (Bat or Vampire Form Only).&nbsp;Melee Weapon Attack:&nbsp;+9&nbsp;to hit, reach 5 ft., one willing creature, or a creature that is&nbsp;grappled&nbsp;by the vampire,&nbsp;incapacitated, or&nbsp;restrained.&nbsp;Hit:&nbsp;7 (1d6 + 4) piercing damage plus 10 (3d6) necrotic damage. The target's hit point maximum is reduced by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. The reduction lasts until the target finishes a long rest. The target dies if this effect reduces its hit point maximum to 0. A humanoid slain in this way and then buried in the ground rises the following night as a vampire spawn under the vampire's control.\n    57→\n    58→## Legendary Actions\n    59→\n    60→The drow can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature&rsquo;s turn. The drow regains spent legendary actions at the start of her turn.\n    61→Demon Staff.&nbsp;The drow makes one attack with her demon staff.\n    62→Compel Demon (Costs 2 Actions).&nbsp;An allied demon within 30 feet of the drow uses its reaction to make one attack against a target of the drow&rsquo;s choice that she can see.\n    63→Cast a Spell (Costs 1&ndash;3 Actions).&nbsp;The drow expends a spell slot to cast a 1st-, 2nd-, or 3rd-level spell that she has prepared. Doing so costs 1 legendary action per level of the spell.Move.&nbsp;The vampire moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks.\n    64→Unarmed Strike.&nbsp;The vampire makes one unarmed strike.\n    65→Bite.(Costs 2 Actions). The vampire makes one bite attack.\n    66→\n    67→"}]},"uuid":"f060b2c7-7b1c-4a34-a2c5-bbcbeb1f016c","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:24.290Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6093a177-3a84-490d-8dfe-17d0cb17f2d3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"552c8efc-3f98-4309-8cfb-97664d02bff5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0118EUDSjPhFCkw9uY1RGvy2","type":"tool_result","content":"<persisted-output>\nOutput too large (67.4KB). Full output saved to: /Users/eviemini/.claude/projects/-Users-eviemini-Documents-code-server-manager/e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4/tool-results/toolu_0118EUDSjPhFCkw9uY1RGvy2.txt\n\nPreview (first 2KB):\n     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"character-timelines\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/character-timelines.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign -- Character Timelines\n     8→\n     9→Reconstructed from player notes (Brigit/Liz chapters 49T-136), DM timeline notes (Acts 1-4), and directory/handout evidence. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→\n    27→### Timeline\n    28→\n    29→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    30→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    31→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by Sabine Ra | [HIGH] Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    32→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | [MEDIUM] Party cohesion building |\n...\n</persisted-output>"}]},"uuid":"8e5bad97-9d29-4d8a-80a8-00c60c62ca94","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:30.456Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"552c8efc-3f98-4309-8cfb-97664d02bff5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
