{"parentUuid":"0e8db876-828d-46d8-8eb4-6d4f23fa3570","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QL6VCwB771ENiSTShFcL5H","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"intrigue-web\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# The Mardov Intrigue Web\n     8→\n     9→*A comprehensive map of every conspiracy, betrayal, hidden agenda, and political thread across 136 chapters.*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Part 1: The Conspiracy Web\n    14→\n    15→### THE COSMIC ENGINE: Vecna vs. Sabine\n    16→\n    17→The entire campaign is driven by one act of divine jealousy. Vecna and Sabine were the original **twin souls** -- both gods of death, but Sabine also ruled life and fate. Vecna's envy of that additional dominion is the cosmic engine driving every conspiracy in Mardov.\n    18→\n    19→Sabine's avatar (the Raven Queen) founded two colleges of magic: the **Circle of the Murder** (necromancy/death, entrusted to her teacher Ramses) and the **Artificer's Guild** (animancy/life, entrusted to her husband Xerxes). Ramses grew jealous and attempted to grant both himself and Sabine eternal life through lichdom. When Sabine rejected the ritual, Ramses forced it on her. Sabine starved her lich form by refusing to consume souls, crystallizing into a Demi-Lich trapped inside a phylactery for centuries.\n    20→\n    21→This created the fracture that Vecna exploits across the entire campaign.\n    22→\n    23→---\n    24→\n    25→### VECNA'S FOUR ZENITHS\n    26→\n    27→Vecna's conquest is organized through four domains of power, each with a lieutenant, a target, and a nested web of agents:\n    28→\n    29→```\n    30→VECNA (God of Evil Secrets, Animancy)\n    31→|\n    32→|-- Commands all four Zenith operations\n    33→|-- Rules V-MARDOV (parallel dimension, mirror of material plane)\n    34→|-- Goal: Destroy all keystones, enable V-MORDRED's invasion\n    35→|\n    36→+-- ZENITH OF DEATH (Forest's Zenith, Machu)\n    37→|   |-- Heart: Heart of the Wilde (Emerald)\n    38→|   |-- Anchor: Anchor of Death (obsidian sword)\n    39→|   |-- Eye: Green Eye of Vecna\n    40→|   |\n    41→|   |-- MORDRED / RAMSES (Primary Antagonist)\n    42→|   |   |-- Phase 1: Lover (wanted to resurrect Sabine)\n    43→|   |   |-- Phase 2: Collector (gathered her gifts after her spirit shattered)\n    44→|   |   |-- Phase 3: Conspirator (convinced Raven Queen avatar to sacrifice\n    45→|   |   |   immortality, creating rift to V-MARDOV; gave up phylactery to\n    46→|   |   |   infiltrate Artificer's Guild; took Xerxes's body)\n    47→|   |   |-- Phase 4: Herald (destroy all keystones for V-MORDRED's invasion)\n    48→|   |   |\n    49→|   |   |-- OPERATIONS:\n    50→|   |   |   |-- Scarlet Tunic Brigade (soul-harvesting front -- 2,000 souls)\n    51→|   |   |   |-- Possessed Finkle Hollingsworth as vessel/disguise\n    52→|   |   |   |-- Deployed Jorgen as lure to recruit adventurers\n    53→|   |   |   |-- Golden Reach (Markus/Danelle, incubus/succubus agents in Argos)\n    54→|   |   |   |-- Hag Coven (Margaret, Gertrude, Tabitha -- brew Pestilence\n    55→|   |   |   |   from corrupted children's souls)\n    56→|   |   |   |-- Mandu (corrupt Argos guard captain, controlled through bribes)\n    57→|   |   |   |-- Reva (Machu Royal Advisor, source of corruption in Machu)\n    58→|   |   |   |-- Tatiana (the Imposter of Machu)\n    59→|   |   |   |-- Jafar (Sally's uncle, hired the Dons to kidnap Sally,\n    60→|   |   |   |   placed Nathan Yanagan as spy on Thornberry Shade)\n    61→|   |   |   |-- Costa Una (traitor within Scarlet Tunic Brigade)\n    62→|   |   |\n    63→|   |   |-- AGENTS: Salina (Death Knight Captain), Lord Soth (Death Knight\n    64→|   |   |   of Vodram)\n    65→|   |\n    66→|   |-- NED'ZAILOK (Lich, Keeper of Death's Anchor in V-MARDOV)\n    67→|   |   |-- Used by Vecna for experiments\n    68→|   |   |-- Betrayed Casaval Dekun (Eladrin warrior)\n    69→|   |   |-- Guards the Death's Zenith in V-MARDOV\n    70→|   |\n    71→|   |-- FOREST GUARDIAN / FOREST TITAN\n    72→|       |-- Awakened by Mordred using the Heart of the Wilde\n    73→|       |-- Boss of Forest's Zenith\n    74→|       |-- Defeating it transports party to V-MARDOV\n    75→|\n    76→+-- ZENITH OF WAR (Mountain's Zenith, Vag Mahar / Underdark)\n    77→|   |-- Heart: Heart of the Forge (Garnet)\n    78→|   |-- Anchor: Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin, Daina's Axe when ascended)\n    79→|   |-- Eye: Red Eye of Vecna\n    80→|   |\n    81→|   |-- PERAXIS PHYREXIA (Illithilich -- THE Central Villain)\n    82→|   |   |-- Origin: Raised by Vecna's power, gifted a shard of the Raven Queen\n    83→|   |   |-- Has all of \"Tiny Rick's\" memories\n    84→|   |   |-- One command from Vecna: \"Assault the War's Zenith\"\n    85→|   |   |-- Corrupted the Elder Brain via Vecna's influence\n    86→|   |   |   (only Ulitharid elite escaped)\n    87→|   |   |\n    88→|   |   |-- OPERATIONS:\n    89→|   |   |   |-- Created the Spell Plague (with Alhoon Ren's research)\n    90→|   |   |   |-- Captured the Stag Zodiac (Shi-Shi-Gami) to locate\n    91→|   |   |   |   Mountain's Zenith\n    92→|   |   |   |-- Holds Thorkon Fireforge captive; plans Ceremorphosis\n    93→|   |   |   |   (mindflayer conversion) as leverage against Daina\n    94→|   |   |   |-- Traded one conversation between Thorkon and Daina for\n    95→|   |   |   |   Thorkon's compliance with Ceremorphosis\n    96→|   |   |   |-- \"Now that we know you're alive, we have a spare\" (Daina)\n    97→|   |   |   |-- Beholder relay network for psychic communication\n    98→|   |   |   |-- Homunculus Thraalm deployed as agent (killed Ch. 66)\n    99→|   |   |   |-- Contacted Daina via beholder relay -- cruel mind Discovered\n   105→|   |       |   vampirism liberates mindflayers from Elder Brain.\n   106→|   |       |   Umber Hulk eyes + Oblivion's blood = individual thought.\n   107→|   |       |   \"Dawn of a new empire\" through Ullitharid Alhoon.\n   108→|   |       |   Secretly taking notes (double agent within own side).\n   109→|   |       |-- Alhoon Pollivar: Runs Umber Hulk research facility.\n   110→|   |       |   Captures gnolls and sailors. Sends converts to Peraxis.\n   111→|   |\n   112→|   |-- OBLIVION WAYNE (Vampire Queen of the Drow)\n   113→|   |   |-- Nocturnus's aunt\n   114→|   |   |-- Married Ebon Wayne (vampire drow) who discovered the Spell Plague\n   115→|   |   |-- Ebon captured the Bat Zodiac; uses \"gift of the first bite\"\n   116→|   |   |   to create alpha vampires\n   117→|   |   |-- Each turned vampire has innate loyalty to Oblivion\n   118→|   |   |-- Used alpha power to depose her brother (Nocturnus's father),\n   119→|   |   |   unite all Drow under vampire rule\n   120→|   |   |-- \"The Drow are no more. Long live the Vampire Queen!\"\n   121→|   |   |-- Obsessed with ancient Warforged army as key to dominating Illithid\n   122→|   |   |-- Plans Drow/Illithid vampire invasion of the overlands\n   123→|   |\n   124→|   |-- GHOST-EYE THORRIN (The Puppetmaster of Vag Mahar)\n   125→|   |   |-- Chief adviser to the Redbeard Thane\n   126→|   |   |-- Convinced Thorkon Fireforge to betray King 140→|   |\n   141→|   |-- THERZADUN (Separate from Vecna but entangled)\n   142→|       |-- Sealed behind Iron Doors at Mountain's Zenith\n   143→|       |-- Reginald Providence (Lich of Therzadun, Maghar of the Belt)\n   144→|       |   |-- Kenku in true form, disguised with plastic surgery\n   145→|       |   |-- Wears cloak of the Raven Queen\n   146→|       |   |-- Killed by Bergs (Ch. 104-105), unleashing Phoenix maul\n   147→|       |-- Grumble (Reginald's apprentice)\n   148→|       |-- Vellem the Beholder (allied with Reginald)\n   149→|\n   150→+-- ZENITH OF PESTILENCE (Winter's Zenith, Bynar)\n   151→|   |-- Heart: Heart of the Faith (Sapphire)\n   152→|   |-- Anchor: Anchor of Pestilence (pan flute / Horn of Winter)\n   153→|   |-- Eye: Blue Eye of Vecna\n   154→| {"parentUuid":"9cc3b3a9-87fe-45ba-8771-8cf8339e5cf5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AhKCjm9ShcXFNaweHiB27a","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: handout\n     3→title: \"Spell Plague Notes 2\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Handouts/Spell Plague Notes 2.txt\"]\n     5→given_to: []\n     6→chapter: \"\"\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→My Dark Lady Wayne,\n    10→\n    11→Your husband's vassal has arrived just in time to see the culmination of my work. \n    12→I look forward to showing Peraxis the benefits that Umber Hulk essence can provide when combined with the gift of your vampirism.\n    13→\n    14→On this day, we celebrate the means for defeating the dwarves and finally may re-claim our rightful place as rulers of all, not just the deep. \n    15→\n    16→Your nephew has been spotted in the area and we have it on good athority that he is fully alive just as the VECNA had ordained. \n    17→I will put the gnomes out on patrol in hopes of squashing this last pathetic reminant of his fathers rule.\n    18→\n    19→The Drow are no more, \n    20→Long live the Vampire Queen!\n    21→\n    22→ALHOON REN\n    23→\n    24→PS: might you ask your husband to allow me to look at the captured zodiac, despite his best efforts it seems that Peraxis is no closer creating a gate to VECNA's petal of existance. \n    25→I am curious if the intel from the other side is correct and that Stag truely can divine the future. \n    26→\n    27→Somehow it helped that Hobgoblin Wretch escape, and the timing was just a bit too perfect. \n    28→"}]},"uuid":"3e715322-9bd9-4d67-b4aa-427d0d6771fd","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:32.289Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9cc3b3a9-87fe-45ba-8771-8cf8339e5cf5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"0e67963b-ee27-46b5-9037-37916c8b3e37","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Loybq8kmu89QAsq9th39GD","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 48T Brigit s Notes The Roost Hotel and Casino teamBattle\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_48T___Brigit_s_Notes___The_Roost_Hotel_and_Casino__teamBattle.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→Notes from Chapter 48T – Brigit’s Notes – The Roost Hotel and Casino #teamBattle\n     8→\n     9→TLDR last time\n    10→\n    11→Keelick transformed into the amulet and is kind of dead?\n    12→\n    13→Brigit was able to help out by being a fart\n    14→\n    15→We were able to get to the eye - a smaller calm area in the giant storm.\n    16→\n    17→However, somehow it went poorly, and we had to fight a roc and some storm giant scouts.\n    18→\n    19→They threw rocs and rocks at us\n    20→\n    21→Ba dum\n    22→\n    23→Tssssssss\n    24→\n    25→The world is really sandy? But in the center of the storm is a birch tree that had been struck by lightning with Druidic language on it - Blood of the first shall open the way, pay the toll and the way shall open.\n    26→\n    27→And we were all transported to the location that was spoken - Cloud City\n    28→\n    29→IN CLOUD CITY\n    30→\n    31→Looking backwards we see a root of a massive pine tree. The roots fall away to reveal each party members.\n    32→\n    33→The ground is two different shades - one is soft but firms up quickly, and the other is large fluffy clouds instead of bushes.\n    34→\n    35→And it is impossible to tell which direction the wind is blowing in cloud city - but there is one wind that seems to overpower all gusts of wind. A moaning of 'My boy' traveling throughout the city\n    36→\n    37→While all of us are looking at a young Aarakocra - hawk based.\n    38→\n    39→He's quite confused, but he looks like he's trying to stay cool.\n    40→\n    41→And there is a flash of black feathers as a small black figure darts behind a building behind our arrival.\n    42→\n    43→Stalwart points this out.\n    44→\n    45→Stalwart checks out the alleyway, Nyx sends out Hedwig to see who the alleyway dude was, and Varys waves to the hawk dude.\n    46→\n    47→And stealthily! Hedwig heads over to the alleyway like a boss. Nyx sees through its eyes and sees a cloaked figure wearing a cloak and carrying a staff. The staff is glowing with a green eldritch light.\n    48→\n    49→Thrometergy\n    50→\n    51→Varys waves to the young Aarakocra who lands in front of us.\n    52→\n    53→And name-drop Keelick.\n    54→\n    55→But we say that we know how Keelick died and he sounds the alarm!\n    56→\n    57→Oh no, we're in trouble.\n    58→\n    59→Looking around, at the top of storm peaks is the temple of the King.\n    60→\n    61→We hear large footsteps coming towards us - Attack on Titan style. They're Hill Giants. Shorter but Wider than other Giants.\n    62→\n    63→SPLITTING THE PARTY\n    64→\n    65→Team Logic/Scaredy Cat/Slum Lord\n    66→\n    67→Nyx, Sprocket, Tezka, Randall and Stalwart are running from the guards and the giant.\n    68→\n    69→They're trying to find an inn to go rest - and they hear violin playing? A specific song\n    70→\n    71→Definitely about us\n    72→\n    73→\"Bard of little talent and lesser skill, your fowl notes are far too shrill\"\n    74→\n    75→\"Sent here by he who's name shall not be said in threes, lost your guide who faded with the breeze\"\n    76→\n    77→\"We do not need you to accomplish our task, you've done enough now turn back\"\n    78→\n    79→\"Little half-elf drop out with a heart that bleeds, turn on your heels and return to your tree\"\n    80→\n    81→And it's coming from the Kenku.\n    82→\n    83→They're heading to the Kenku\n    84→\n    85→And the hawk is also following us\n    86→\n    87→Team Diplomat/Bout to be Flat\n    88→\n    89→Varys, Fen and Brigit want an audience with a king\n    90→\n    91→The Hill Giant is barreling over \"INVADERS\"\n    92→\n    93→Brigit kind of understands him in Druidic? And tries to reason with him, but he is not having it.\n    94→\n    95→Fen tries to get an audience with the storm king\n    96→\n    97→He drops his weapon which Brigit dodges and reaches for Fen and Varys\n    98→\n    99→Varys misty steps away and Fen just gets taken.\n   100→\n   101→We're trying to be taken willingly, Fen is being held in front of the giant and Varys and Brigit follow behind.\n   102→\n   103→Team Logic/Scaredy Cat/Slum Lord\n   104→\n   105→The Kenku darts into a building\n   106→\n   107→And the Aarakocra runs into the side of a building\n   108→\n   109→Nyx is going to change everyone in the group into different Aarakocra. Stalwart is a hawk, Nyx is an owl, Sprocket is a penguin, Tezka is a hummingbird, and Randall is a mockingbird!\n   110→\n   111→Randall throws some SHADE at the Kenku\n   112→\n   113→I hear your song despite the wording\n   114→\n   115→I can hear your poor throat is hurting\n   116→\n   117→A child's rhyme full of snark\n   118→\n   119→Your master sent you here to bark\n   120→\n   121→We have better things to do\n   122→\n   123→So notify me when you're through\n   124→\n   125→(https://media3.giphy.com/media/82IQ2RSoFRxO8RYzp7/giphy.gif?cid=6104955e5c92e65d4672613851346dd6)\n   126→\n   127→And a door opens! The Kenku beckons for Randall to follow!\n   128→\n   129→But first, check if the hawk fell for the distraction. Two older Aarakocra - a pigeon and a macaw bonk him on the head and they shove him in the door of the building - a huge manor\n   130→\n   131→TEAM BIRD\n   132→\n   133→Everyone goes with Randall\n   134→\n   135→They all get a good look at the Kenku creature - Randall then Tezka, then everyone else. Hummingbird Tezka walks in and gets squawked at by the Kenku.\n   136→\n   137→Sprocket casts Comprehend Languages - The Kenku introduces itself as EEP.\n   138→\n   139→And there's also a pigeon woman that greets the party\n   140→\n   141→The party has walked into a café, but through the backdoor in the kitchen.\n   142→\n   143→Stalwart walks around the counter, out the front door and then back in.\n   144→\n   145→They want drinks and a room.\n   146→\n   147→And also offers a map that can be bought.\n   148→\n   149→But the café owner says they need Electra money to buy things.\n   150→\n   151→But they don't have it! And it's strange that they don't, because they look like they've live here….\n   152→\n   153→The pigeon woman, V, saves them and gets the café owner to leave.\n   154→\n   155→V wonders why they were in her dreams\n   156→\n   157→But they didn't look like this. And there were more of us…\n   158→\n   159→Stalwart insights V. There is something strange about her - she speaks with an upper-class cadence, she throws around money, but her clothing is all poorly handmade. It seems as though she is under the influence of a charmed person.\n   160→\n   161→Reynaldine?\n   162→\n   163→Team Diplomat/Bout to be Flat\n   164→\n   165→As we're walking through, we notice there aren't many guards. They're storm giants.\n   166→\n   167→The guards we're passing don't see us, and the giant tells us to be quiet.\n   168→\n   169→Brigit casts Pass without a trace on herself and Varys and we continue following.\n   170→\n   171→We quickly listen to the guards - the idiot bellboy, always up to something.\n   172→\n   173→And then he skips along.\n   174→\n   175→Brigit sticks around for more information, and Varis tries to keep up.\n   176→\n   177→Brigit hears - man I wish we weren't on guard duty, there's something cool going on - intruders. And he heard there was a red dragon about.\n   178→\n   179→It might be a fire genasi.\n   180→\n   181→And she sees an owl with a brown coat and a mustache - well dressed.\n   182→\n   183→The owl is looking for the hill giant!\n   184→\n   185→Mr. Tux will hear of this! And the brick is dotted with gold, a golden road. No problem.\n   244→\n   245→It's 300 Electrum an evening. 1500 Gold -> 300 Electrum\n   246→\n   247→Mr. Tux is SUPER excited that they're paying in gold. BEAST\n   384→\n   385→The Sky Whales are headed to canvas made buildings that are held taut by the wind. {"parentUuid":"e60e09a6-1dad-42df-995b-de37af362398","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012vKh8UD9jKApRvffF9hj4F","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Terrell Temptus, The Storm King\"\n     4→source: [\"World Anvil\"]\n     5→world_anvil_category: \"\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# Terrell Temptus, The Storm King\n     9→\n    10→Air genasi king and father of Kelik \"Waystar\" Temptus, a friend of the party from their days aboard the Thornberry Wilde.\n    11→Terrell is a veteran of the last of the Elemental Wars, during which split his people's allegiance with the\n    12→Autumnborn\n    13→and sided with the Fire Genasi after learning of the recent submergence of the Citadel of the Sun into the new ocean which was created at the end of the Reign of Fire. Him and his people managed to capture the Water Genasi Princess, Lapis Poseidon along with her peoples Elemental Seal. After the war, the Air Genasi kept their prisoner of war due to the Water Genasi refusing to give up the now drowned Sunless Citadel. To punish the Drowned Throne, Lapis and her amulet were to be held by the Air Genasi Kingdom as reassurance that they never move to attack them.\n    14→Was betrayed by his political Wife and Queen, Thestra, the Storm Giant. Her overwhelming power granted to her in exchange for a newfound partnership with her long lost Fire Giant kin. The last of the Temptus line after the demise of his son at the hands of the Abomination that used to be his Leader, Reynardine of the Autumn Equinox.\n    15→For winning the the last Elemental War, Temptus won his Fey Lord the Autumn Equinox, and his Fire Genasi Allies won the Spring Equinox for\n    16→The Queen of Thorns\n    17→. Their lords used their newfound titles to claim their respective courts. However Juliannia the night of her sister's coronation and Reynardine the night of his own stole off in the night, after Summer's Last Light, Only the newly formed Reynaldine returned...\n    18→Temptus was never granted the respect and recognition he saught from his new master and allowed the Aarkocra refugee Euclid sanctuary, in exchange for Reynaldine of the Autumn Court to be banned from his kingdom. Among\n    19→Cloud City\n    20→,\n    21→Leonidas, Now Leonidas of Summer's Last Light\n    22→of the newly formed\n    23→WindHaven\n    24→would join Euclid of\n    25→Alexandria\n    26→and Temptus with the forbiddence. The Harvest Festival of Windhaven is in celebration of this event. It occurs on the Fall Equinox in one final jab at their so-called new boss by stealing a portion of the significance of his title.\n    27→"}]},"uuid":"5ae56579-d708-45d4-86e1-4f7a5c0bc766","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:39.764Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e60e09a6-1dad-42df-995b-de37af362398","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"97b5d76a-ea9b-4f01-9b82-830cb6ed9a31","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K83RCpe9ySXswANutmzFCZ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: creature\n     3→name: \"Archimedes\"\n     4→source: [\"D&D Beyond Homebrew\"]\n     5→ac: 14\n     6→hp: 40\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→# Archimedes\n    10→\n    11→**AC** 14 | **HP** 40\n    12→**Stats**: STR 14 | DEX 17 | CON 14 | INT 10 | WIS 16 | CHA 12\n    13→**Senses**: Darkvision 120 ft.\n    14→**Skills**: Stealth +4, Perception +5\n    15→\n    16→\n    17→## Special Traits\n    18→\n    19→Flyby. The owl doesn't provoke opportunity attacks when it flies out of an enemy's reach.\n    20→Keen Hearing and Sight. The owl has advantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on hearing or sight.\n    21→Astral Form.&nbsp;The owl has the ability to travel on the Ethereal Plane, however the owl may only occupy places where it could travel on the Material Plane. It is a bonus action to switch forms. If the owl is grappling a Large or smaller and has moved more than half their speed prior to shifting, the owl may bring the grappled creature with them to the Ethereal Plane. The strain causes the grappled to make a DC 15 Str Saving Throw or else take 2d10 force damage. Once shifted the owl must be able to move an additional 10ft or else both the owl and any passenger must make DC 16 Const Saving Throws else take 2d10 + 2 force damage.\n    22→\n    23→## Actions\n    24→\n    25→Talons. Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. {"parentUuid":"d009b5d4-e899-4c45-9d8e-c68bb39f8203","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HGbk9PpD3qHzGN6QUR5wMi","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"character-timelines\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/character-timelines.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign -- Character Timelines\n     8→\n     9→Reconstructed from player notes (Brigit/Liz chapters 49T-136), DM timeline notes (Acts 1-4), and directory/handout evidence. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→\n    27→### Timeline\n    28→\n    29→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    30→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    31→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by Sabine Ra | [HIGH] Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    32→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | [MEDIUM] Party cohesion building |\n    33→| 13 | Liberation of Argos | [MEDIUM] First major quest completion |\n    34→| 30-37 | Underdark descent | [MEDIUM] Party faces darkness together |\n    35→| 42 | Campaign splits -- Brigit goes with Tieflings track | [MEDIUM] Party division begins |\n    36→| 49T | Cloud City heist: summons 8 boars to rescue Randall from casino, discovers fiends beneath The Roost, Fen is charmed by Minnie the succubus | [HIGH] First Cloud City arc; Brigit shows tactical creativity |\n    37→| 64T | Harvest Festival preparations: brews beer and wine with the party (Harvest Day IPA, Return of Summer Marigold Wine), creates bottle labels | [HIGH] Distillery business origins |\n    38→| 64T | Discovers drab with sun tattoo, takes pendant | [HIGH] Investigating the drab plague |\n    39→| 69T | Battles the 6-fingered Necromancer (Ned'Zailok) at the Westrun scarecrow farm, fights to burn effigies and save zodiac spirits | [HIGH] First direct confrontation with major villain |\n    40→| 69T | Dies in battle, revived by Varis' lingering Healing Spirit | [HIGH] First death (not counting Ch 2) |\n    41→| 69T | Whisked to Eden after revival | [HIGH] First contact with progenitor realm |\n    42→| 72T-76T | Alabaster Seat tournament: teams with Anemone, fights beholders, navigates political intrigue, rescues Ruth and Sheila from Mage's Prison | [HIGH] Distillery political arc |\n    43→| 77T | Trial of Sprocket: testifies in court, casts Greater Restoration on Lilith to break spellbook curse | [HIGH] Proves loyalty to party |\n    44→| 78T | Beholder trial: teams with Anemone and Olivia, Anemone destroys the beholder in 2 rounds | [HIGH] Combat prowess of allies |\n    45→| 79T | Feywilde journey: attends Summer Court tea party, votes in the House of Blood | [HIGH] Feywilde politics |\n    46→| 82T | Becomes a Progenitor through ritual in Eden with Children of the Forest | [HIGH] Major transformation -- one of the campaign's pivotal character moments |\n    47→| 82T | Finds Finkle Hollingsworth in the Eavallan Forest, fights him, chases through tree stride to Temple of Life and Death in Argos | [HIGH] Finkle has Heart of the Wilde in his eye |\n    48→| 83T | Flies 200 miles to Distillery as Quetzalcoatl to retrieve bag of holding, flies back | [HIGH] Shows dedication and power |\n    49→| 84T | Discovers underground city beneath Argos, rescues 4 Children of the Forest (total 8/12 saved) | [HIGH] Children of the Forest quest progress |\n    50→| 85T | Battle of the Distillery: turns into dragon, uses Call Lightning against Dallor | [HIGH] Major battle |\n    51→| 86 | REUNION: full party together, fights Dallor, Sprig shatters the crystalline heart | [HIGH] End of Act 3 split |\n    52→| 87T | Performs Ritual of Reincarnation for Freld and 6 others; Daina receives Heart of the Mountain | [HIGH] Progenitor powers in action |\n    53→| 88-89 | Mourning period: becomes a wolf, creates barbarian camp, regrows burned forest | [HIGH] Processing grief |\n    54→| 90 | Explores Machu and House of Life and Death; captured briefly, HAL rescues her and Cersei | [HIGH] Temple of Life and Death reconnaissance |\n    55→| 91T-99 | Distillery period: business operations, defense building, STB confrontation | [MEDIUM] Settling and fortifying |\n    56→| 100 | Shadowfell journey -- encounters time dilation | [HIGH] Major planar adventure |\n    57→| 101 | Mountain disappears, summoned to Court of Bones | [HIGH] Escalating cosmic events |\n    58→| 103 | The Reunion (third time) | [HIGH] Party comes together again |\n    59→| 104-105 | Witnesses Anchor of War events, Daina ascends | [HIGH] Supporting role in critical moment |\n    60→| 107 | Ragnarok / Threads of Fate | [HIGH] Cosmic battle |\n    61→| 108 | Weaver of Lies / Arachnophobia | [HIGH] Lolth encounter |\n    62→| 112 | Crystallization; portals; \"Rube GoldBERGS\" | [HIGH] Sprocket's condition worsens |\n    63→| 113 | Sacrifices / Matter of Life and Death | [HIGH] Major moral choices |\n    64→| 115B | Darkeyes / Wild Magic and Monologues | [HIGH] Fen's confrontation arc |\n    65→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal | [HIGH] Plot threads collide |\n    66→| 117 | Kindness / Through the Membrane Glass / Therapy | [HIGH] Emotional processing |\n    67→| 121 | Stalwart corruption/redemption duels; Brigit ascends the locket (Anchor of Death) | [HIGH] The locket's power is fully realized |\n    68→| 125 | Varis retrieves final Asmodeus seal | [HIGH] Endgame preparation |\n    69→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey battle: Brigit is cleansed by Narcissa's rapier, goes full rage as Lox, fights temple leaders | [HIGH] \"I live with this shit every goddamn day\" -- iconic rage speech |\n    70→| 128 | Uncleansed after fight; rapier returns to Josh | [HIGH] Cleansing didn't stick; identity remains complex |\n    71→| 130 | Cloud City return: disguises as eagle aaracokra, helps heist Lapis from throne room, frees the 10th Wind | [HIGH] Major heist sequence |\n    72→| 131 | Cloud City continued: creates wall of fire corridors, rescues civilians, turns into giant eagle to carry party | [HIGH] Tactical and heroic |\n    73→| 132 | Stone Table ritual: fights alongside party, casts cone of cold at Gabriel, heals Bergs as zodiac of life | [HIGH] Critical battle |\n    74→| 133 | Harvest: uses scroll of Transport via Plants to reach Conquis Abbey, turns into Quetzalcoatl to reach zenith | [HIGH] Rapid deployment |\n    75→| 135 | Divinity: casts Bestow Curse on Asmodeus, fights in dragon form, casts Haste on self, channels Nivens for stealth | [HIGH] Multi-form combat mastery |\n    76→| 135 | Scries on Forest Zenith anchor room: sees demi-lich phylactery and zombie Finkle | [HIGH] Discovers Forest Zenith's state |\n    77→| 136 | Battlefield: locket pulsating with acrid green (usually emerald), rain of ash at Machu, elemental powers gone | [HIGH] Power loss -- voices gone, alone in her head for first time |\n    78→| 136 | Speaks with dead on black dragon; learns Vecna is on Mardov, wants Death Zenith | [HIGH] Critical intelligence |\n    79→\n    80→### Turning Points\n    81→\n    82→1. **Death and Revival (Ch 69T)**: First real death, revived by party magic. Establishes mortality stakes.\n    83→2. **Becoming a Progenitor (Ch 82T)**: Transforms from adventurer to something more -- connected to the land itself.\n    84→3. **The Rage Speech (Ch 127)**: \"Do you have any idea how many voices exist in my head at any given time?\" -- defines her identity.\n    85→4. **Power Loss (Ch 135-136)**: Elemental magic stripped at Harvest Zenith. The silence in her head is deafening.\n    86→\n    87→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n    88→\n    89→- Alive, powers stripped (no elemental/vegan magic)\n    90→- Anchor of Death ascended but forest zenith situation unclear\n    91→- Locket pulsating abnormally (acrid green vs usual emerald)\n    92→- Must find Forest Zenith and confront zombie Finkle + demi-lich phylactery\n    93→- Needs her powers turned back on\n    94→\n    95→---\n    96→\n    97→## SPROCKET OVERSPARK\n    98→\n    99→**Player**: (unknown)\n   100→**Class**: Wizard (School of Divination)"}]},"uuid":"4f3f24c1-5579-4dc0-9894-0bec7de5fb0d","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:40.357Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d009b5d4-e899-4c45-9d8e-c68bb39f8203","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"aafc2ad8-3523-4d3e-ac03-7e510b62035e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UjEuvEmLovQF4P6GEG2PWA","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 38X - Nyx s notes - Euclid s Study 1\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_38X_-_Nyx_s_notes_-_Euclid_s_Study_1_.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→Notes from Chapter 38X – Euclid’s Study\n     8→\n     9→(These were relayed from Nyx and Stalwart)\n    10→\n    11→Suddenly, Stalwart and Nyx are pulled through a portal!\n    12→\n    13→It's different from our normal portals, more jarring.\n    14→\n    15→They also see visions of an owl. Linda's mimicking, and Hedwig is nowhere to be found\n    16→\n    17→They land!\n    18→\n    19→And are greeted by an owl humanoid creature amongst a pile of books.\n    20→\n    21→Apparently he only wanted one of them.\n    22→\n    23→They're in a huge library, glowing blue somehow.\n    24→\n    25→Behind the owl is a window blinking yellow-white and from there, a huge voice telling Euclid to calm down, and Inchu has asked their help to reclaim the Sunless Citadel - they need to clear out the snakes\n    26→\n    27→Euclid calls this voice Archimedes, and says Nyx (the Tome-Sworn) is on thin ice.\n    28→\n    29→Euclid was the keeper of the Divine Seal, but he is no longer.\n    30→\n    31→His enemy is also Reynaldine of the Autumn Court.\n    32→\n    33→Reynaldine\n    34→\n    35→Apparently his powers are not entirely his own\n    36→\n    37→He was once a Prince of Harvest\n    38→\n    39→Now, he's the Ruler of the Feywilde West of the Glittering Run\n    40→\n    41→And those under the Queen of Thorns call him an Abomination.\n    42→\n    43→Be careful though, we might not want this information - for knowledge is power.\n    44→\n    45→He might not know, but he'll know that Euclid talked to Nyx.\n    46→\n    47→All the Lore!\n    48→\n    49→7 heavens were ruled by 3 entities: Barachiel the Just, Dominel the Righteous and Gabriel the Kind. They were gifted domain of at least one celestial plane.\n    50→\n    51→Prime: Gabriel.\n    52→\n    53→9 Hells - Lords of the Nine and the Princes of the Abyss. And they had a blood war over it and Asmodeus's seals.\n    54→\n    55→Nobody thankfully is stupid enough to release him.\n    56→\n    57→Conflict:\n    58→\n    59→2 fated betrothed in love with beings above them.\n    60→\n    61→Reynaldine and Juliannia Princess of the Summer Solstice\n    62→\n    63→Gabriel the Rind and Graz'zt.\n    64→\n    65→Juliannia was bewitched by Graz'zt, and elevated the Ambitious Devil into a demon, Juliannia becoming his Demon Amulet.\n    66→\n    67→Graz'zt became the Demon Prince the Lord of Pleasure.\n    68→\n    69→Gabriel condensed himself and the Demon Prince into Graz'zt's Demonic Amulet\n    70→\n    71→Reynaldine took the amulet, twisting himself.\n    72→\n    73→He was never forgiven by most, especially the Queen of Thorns, Evelyandrin of the Spring Court, sister of Juliannia.\n    74→\n    75→And yes, it was Reynaldine's fault. He snuck off to meet with Gabriel and caused Juliannia to search for him.\n    76→\n    77→Present time\n    78→\n    79→Reynaldine took Gabriel's Divine Seal and caused the collapse of the Celestial Realm, and further collapsed the 9 Hells.\n    80→\n    81→They aren't gone, but they've merged into the FeyWilde.\n    82→\n    83→Most citizens are held captive in a ruined human city in the feywilde\n    84→\n    85→ALSO His Origins of Genasi book is missing.\n    86→\n    87→Why us?\n    88→\n    89→Not sure.\n    90→\n    91→But! Reynaldine may have 4 pacts within his 4 selves. Nyx's pact may be with Juliannia.\n    92→\n    93→Also, Reynaldine did make the pact with Tygras that began this whole Tiefling nonsense (probably Graz'zt)\n    94→\n    95→Nyx's sister, Xyn, might have a pact with Gabriel\n    96→\n    97→And Reynaldine himself might be saving his pact.\n    98→\n    99→The Queen of Thorns is within the Feywilde, her kingdom almost gone as the Autumn Court's forces have pushed them into the briar patch\n   100→\n   101→Do not mention the theatre, her sister. AND NO RED.\n   102→\n   103→The goblin tribes were lured and sacrificed to the great serpent, but there should be a few outliers about.\n   104→\n   105→Goals!\n   106→\n   107→Euclid believes it possible to bring Juliannia back with Gabriel. But we'll need Xyn and Tygras, and Reynaldine's pactbound.\n   108→"}]},"uuid":"518cb646-97b1-45b3-91c2-fa04ecede3d4","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:48.658Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"aafc2ad8-3523-4d3e-ac03-7e510b62035e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Vag Mahar where Nocturnus had been pulled through the keyhole and captured by Lolth, The Weaver of Fate. The party finds Lilly of Goodbarrel outside, clearly scared of being in the crosshairs of another God, she is sent to help the Dwarven Resistance effort at the Mines. From under the night sky, the party can see the shattered Emerald Veil and evidence of the Elemental Evils headed to the Convergence in the middle of the Serpent Sea.\n    88→\n    89→Bergs unveils his new Peaceforger and inspires the party with his first speech!\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→### Slide 8\n    94→\n    95→New Landmass Who Dis?\n    96→\n    97→---\n    98→\n    99→### Slide 9\n   100→"}]},"uuid":"2bdf7d4f-ee30-4376-9898-2b0236959fd0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:49.808Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a205beb3-6cbf-42bf-8ae9-9f337fc76c90","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"cd8a7c38-6076-42d6-b42a-f6758f0750fa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01X5iseBsbKN39Vv4STtrVTk","type":"tool_result","content":"   289→## NOCTURNUS WAYNE\n   290→\n   291→**Player**: (unknown)\n   292→**Class**: Monk (Way of Shadow) / multiclass\n   293→**Race**: Drow\n   294→**Key Items**: Bane sword, Destiny/Honor/Fate swords\n   295→**Track**: Dwarves -> Craftkeepers -> Good Guys Guild (with solo tangents)\n   296→\n   297→### Arc Summary\n   298→\n   299→Nocturnus is the last scion of House Wayne, a Drow noble family destroyed when his aunt Oblivion Wayne gave her soul to Tiamat. His arc is about reclaiming his heritage, curing his vampirism, and fulfilling the Darkest Night prophecy. He operates as a lone wolf -- often making separate plans from the party -- until his destiny is fulfilled at the Harvest Zenith. After absorbing a portion of Asmodeus's essence upon the god's death, he fully ascends, gains Promise (originator of the Darkest Night prophecy) as his companion, and must now rule the Underdark.\n   300→\n   301→### Timeline\n   302→\n   303→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   304→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   305→| 0-41 | United party; backstory of aunt Oblivion and House Wayne destruction | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   306→| 42-86 | Dwarves track: navigating Vag'Mahar politics (three Thane sectors secretly run by same mind flayer) | [MEDIUM] Political arc |\n   307→| 109 | Cured of vampirism by Josh | [HIGH] Major personal milestone |\n   308→| 109 | Receives Bane sword | [HIGH] New weapon |\n   309→| 115B | Darkest Night prophecy articulated | [HIGH] Destiny defined |\n   310→| 130 | Makes separate plan to walk up beanstalk to Cloud City while party orchestrates heist | [HIGH] Classic lone wolf behavior |\n   311→| 130 | Keelick gives him Honor sword; Nocturnus promises to take on Tiamat alone | [HIGH] Sworn to impossible task |\n   312→| 130 | \"Toss me\" -- rides Keelick's sword throw at Tiamat | [HIGH] Iconic moment |\n   313→| 131 | Stabs Tiamat while riding on her back with Bergs | [HIGH] Fulfilling promise |\n   314→| 131 | Rides Tiamat down to convergence with the 10th Wind and Keelick | [HIGH] Strategic sacrifice |\n   315→| 132 | With Bergs fighting Tiamat; sent to convergence | [MEDIUM] Supporting role |\n   316→| 133 | Wanted Honor sword (party said no); established as complex ally | [HIGH] Tension with party |\n   317→| 135 | Attacks Tiamat, hit by all 5 heads, split in half, dies | [HIGH] Prophecy death |\n   318→| 135 | Revivified by Varis; this is what Shattar Kai do -- die destined deaths and are revived | [HIGH] Resurrection fulfills destiny |\n   319→| 135 | Uses Empty Body (monk ability), takes Drow form, looks much younger | [HIGH] Transformation |\n   320→| 135 | Absorbs third spectre of Asmodeus's death essence; \"I FEEL LIKE A GOD\" | [HIGH] Power ascension |\n   321→| 135 | Levels up twice from absorbed power; Sprocket sees horror on his face | [HIGH] Ominous power gain |\n   322→| 135 | Sword Fate \"shines and bleeds, threads of fate unravel\" | [HIGH] Sword transforms |\n   323→| 136 | Corrals Tiamat's worm-heads with fire damage, finishes kills, fully ascends | [HIGH] Peak power |\n   324→| 136 | Promise emerges from pool of red liquid: \"My liege, I swear to you. You've fulfilled the darkest night prophecy.\" | [HIGH] Prophecy complete |\n   325→| 136 | Must return to Underdark to rule with Promise | [HIGH] New destiny |\n   326→| 136 | Party notes: \"The party has now traveled with both kings of the underdark: Bergs and Nocturnus\" | [HIGH] Dual kingship |\n   327→\n   328→### Turning Points\n   329→\n   330→1. **Cured of Vampirism (Ch 109)**: Josh's act of kindness frees him.\n   331→2. **Honor Sword and Tiamat (Ch 130)**: Accepts impossible solo mission.\n   332→3. **Death and Revival (Ch 135)**: Split in half by Tiamat, revived -- fulfills Shattar Kai destiny.\n   333→4. **Promise Emerges (Ch 136)**: Darkest Night prophecy complete; gains his queen.\n   334→\n   335→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n   336→\n   337→- Alive; ascended through Asmodeus essence + Shattar Kai revival\n   338→- Has Promise as companion/queen\n   339→- Must return to Underdark to rule\n   340→- Wields transformed sword(s)\n   341→- Crystalline heart still present\n   342→\n   343→---\n   344→\n   345→## FEN (DARKEYES)\n   346→\n   347→**Player**: (unknown)\n   348→**Class**: Ranger (with fey/patron connections)\n   349→**Race**: Half-elf\n   350→**Key Items**: Ring of Mind Shielding, Mahen (spirit companion), Fang (bow, sought)\n   351→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n   352→\n   353→### Arc Summary\n   354→\n   355→Fen is the party's other ranger, deeply connected to the natural and spirit world through Mahen (his companion hawk). His arc is defined by loss -- losing his psychic connection to Mahen when she sacrifices her essence to save him from The Sleepless, losing control over his own destiny as gods and fey lords use him as a pawn. He becomes the Knight of the Twisted Meadow and delivers the Heart of Faith (Ch 105), but grows increasingly disillusioned. His journey through Yuri's portal to Uvan represents his search for autonomy.\n   356→\n   357→### Timeline\n   358→\n   359→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   360→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   361→| 0-41 | United party; establishes ranger/naturalist role | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   362→| 42-51 | Tieflings track; solo adventure (Ch 51) | [MEDIUM] Party split |\n   363→| 49T | Charmed by Minnie (succubus) at Cloud City: stunned, mind-controlled, given false memory of Gar'daemon, trigger phrase \"when the jack falls, you charge\" | [HIGH] Horrifying manipulation |\n   364→| 64T | Gets ride to Cloud City for one day (Storm Peaks library research), learns about Gar'daemon and zodiacs | [HIGH] Lore acquisition |\n   365→| 69T | Fights Necromancer: fires arrow through 6-fingered hand and chest, kills Ned'Zailok | [HIGH] Delivers killing blow to major villain |\n   366→| 72T-76T | Tournament: partnered with Sil, encounters Ring of Mind Shielding with Argent (bat zodiac spirit), enters Mage's Prison | [HIGH] Political arc |\n   367→| 75T | Mage's Prison: grappled by Bigsby's Hand, casts Silence on Necromancer, facilitates Sprocket's escape with Ruth | [HIGH] Clutch tactical play |\n   368→| 77T | Kills Leon with Lightning Arrow: \"blinding white light covers the room, Leon is standing there charred\" | [HIGH] Ends the trial villain |\n   369→| 78T | Beholder trial: fires first shots, coordinates with team | [MEDIUM] Combat role |\n   370→| 79T | Feywilde: gives ring to Varis (Argent inside), interacts with Yuri | [HIGH] Spirit politics |\n   371→| 82T | Plane of Water: loses psychic connection to Mahen when she sacrifices her essence to save him from The Sleepless | [HIGH] Devastating personal loss |\n   372→| 83T | Goes to Goldentooth village (Stalwart's orphanage); encounters Sandra (Yanti child); threatens the Yanti | [HIGH] Aggressive response to manipulation |\n   373→| 84T | Returns to Distillery via portal; finds tower under siege by zombies and undead | [HIGH] Homecoming to war |\n   374→| 85T | Defends Distillery solo: swims through zombie-infested bay, reaches grove, summons Sidereal Vizier which resets the zombie clock | [HIGH] Solo heroism |\n   375→| 86 | Reunion: operates gatling gun, coaches Devon, reconnects with brother | [HIGH] Family reunion |\n   376→| 87T | Learns about Fang (his bow, mentioned 3 times by different people); Yanti still seeking Karina | [HIGH] Quest hooks |\n   377→| 88-89 | Becomes disillusioned: \"tired of fighting, tired of being brought into fights from gods\" | [HIGH] Character crisis |\n   378→| 91T | Enters Yuri's portal to Uvan; gets his eyes back; told to find someone with a mask who needs a silver blade | [HIGH] Solo quest |\n   379→| 91T | Returns from Uvan at same moment he left (time displacement) | [HIGH] Temporal anomaly |\n   380→| 105 | Delivers Heart of Faith | [HIGH] Major quest completion |\n   381→| 115B-116 | Confronts Aboleth/Sleepless, convergence and betrayal | [HIGH] Nemesis confrontation |\n   382→\n   383→### Turning Points\n   384→\n   385→1. **Charmed at Cloud City (Ch 49T)**: Demonstrates vulnerability to mental control.\n   386→2. **Killing the Necromancer (Ch 69T)**: Arrow through hand and chest -- decisive heroism.\n   387→3. **Losing Mahen (Ch 82T)**: The psychic bond broken; defining loss.\n   388→4. **Disillusionment (Ch 88-89)**: Questioning the entire enterprise; \"tired of being a pawn.\"\n   389→\n   390→### Status (as of latest notes)\n   391→\n   392→- Alive, eyes restored (from Uvan journey)\n   393→- Mahen partially reconnected (returned to temple, made nest)\n   394→- Knight of the Twisted Meadow\n   395→- Seeking Fang (the bow)\n   396→- Still navigating Yanti demands\n   397→\n   398→---\n   399→\n   400→## DAINA / DIESA FIREFORGE\n   401→\n   402→**Player**: (unknown)\n   403→**Class**: Barbarian / Warforged hybrid\n   404→**Race**: Dwarf (Forgesworn heritage, reincarnated as Duergar)\n   405→**Key Items**: Heart of the Mountain, Warforged network, Anchor of War\n   406→**Track**: Dwarves -> Craftkeepers -> Good Guys Guild\n   407→\n   408→### Arc Summary"}]},"uuid":"a84bd92d-0fb2-4f61-9334-029b0e34bd52","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:54.443Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cd8a7c38-6076-42d6-b42a-f6758f0750fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Rex]] in prayer to [[Bahamut]], the party encounters horrors in the Underdark: a Dueregar woman bearing [[Tiamat]]'s symbol who attacks with a wall of wind, a young drow boy seeking sanctuary from his possessed older brother, and a Mind Flayer that invades their minds.\n    20→\n    21→The pivotal discovery is a teleportation circle in a cramped study. Sleeping within it triggers a vision of an army of Dueregar stretching as far as the eye can see, worshipping a deity in unison. [[Tiberious D. Rex|Tiberious]] recognizes this as evidence of the final war and departs to alert the Silver Scales clergy, leaving the party once more.\n    22→\n    23→## Key Events\n    24→\n    25→- Dueregar [[Tiamat]] worshipper encountered\n    26→- Mind Flayer encounter (psychic attacks)\n    27→- Teleportation circle discovered in Underdark\n    28→- Apocalyptic vision: massive Dueregar army worshipping unknown deity\n    29→- [[Tiberious D. Rex|Tiberious]] departs to alert the clergy about the coming war\n    30→\n    31→## Locations Visited\n    32→\n    33→- Underdark tunnels\n    34→- Dueregar chambers\n    35→\n    36→## NPCs Encountered\n    37→\n    38→- [[Tiberious D. {"parentUuid":"49e3cccb-65f4-44cf-badd-a7b6a38296ea","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PYH6PACKRLyMJ5fbRcBW4D","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n     7→\n     8→## The Overarching Story\n     9→\n    10→The campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n    11→\n    12→1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n    13→2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n    14→3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    15→\n    16→### The Keystones\n    17→\n    18→| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n    19→|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n    20→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n    21→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n    22→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n    23→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n    28→\n    29→The party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n    30→\n    31→**Key beats:**\n    32→- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n    33→- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n    34→- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n    35→- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n    40→\n    41→The party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n    42→\n    43→**Key beats:**\n    44→- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n    45→- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n    46→- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n    47→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n    48→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→\n    52→## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n    53→\n    54→Campaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n    55→\n    56→### The Cloud City Arc ([[windhaven]])\n    57→\n    58→The party arrives in [[windhaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n    59→\n    60→1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n    61→2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n    62→3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n    63→   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n    64→   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n    65→   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n    66→   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n    67→   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n    68→4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n    69→5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n    70→6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n    71→7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n    72→8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n    73→9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    74→10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n    79→\n    80→The threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n    81→\n    82→**Key beats:**\n    83→- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n    84→- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n    85→- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n    86→- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n    87→- [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n    88→- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n    89→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## Global Conflicts\n    94→\n    95→### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n    96→\n    97→**Location:** [[dawnpass]]/[[windhaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n    98→\n    99→**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[uma]].\n   100→"}]},"uuid":"cf2f22fc-72ea-43c7-9e65-0a544291de0c","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:56.283Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"49e3cccb-65f4-44cf-badd-a7b6a38296ea","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"a60e3ae6-f3b8-4645-8c4d-a9e15ac03d03","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01RSfpoMPeRt1vwVy8oWJafS","toolUseID":"toolu_01RSfpoMPeRt1vwVy8oWJafS","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:01.920Z","uuid":"92fb0f59-fe89-4565-b92c-a0eac0ce95ac","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"92fb0f59-fe89-4565-b92c-a0eac0ce95ac","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01RSfpoMPeRt1vwVy8oWJafS","toolUseID":"toolu_01RSfpoMPeRt1vwVy8oWJafS","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:01.920Z","uuid":"af009ae2-330e-4ed0-84d6-1e938e157c34","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"a60e3ae6-f3b8-4645-8c4d-a9e15ac03d03","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RSfpoMPeRt1vwVy8oWJafS","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: handout\n     3→title: \"Spell Plague Notes 1\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Handouts/Spell Plague Notes 1.txt\"]\n     5→given_to: []\n     6→chapter: \"\"\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→My Dark Lady, \n    10→\n    11→The origin of the spell plague is nothing short of pure genious. \n    12→I do not know this new husband of yours, but I would very much so like to shake the hand of the man who discovered such a terribly efficient affliction.\n    13→\n    14→It seems that without the Heart of the Faith, even humans are starting to be affected, although without a hivemind, humanity seems to instead undergo some crisis of faith, \n    15→clearly there is more to the origin of this disease than you have let on. It has become impossible to tell what the original purpose of this plague was for. \n    16→\n    17→I have been taking careful notes of my findings and have noticed that the plague seems to have an unknown origin. In all my days, I've never seen anything like this. \n    18→Could it be from another plane?\n    19→Have you heard anything from the Bat zodiac? Perhaps he would be \"willing\" to part with that information :) \n    20→\n    21→I have been researching how to rid ourselves of this pesky air of resentment that remains in so many of our bretherin. \n    22→Do they not know we are on the verge of greatness? It seems that those who were born illithid are desperate to maintain that connection. \n    23→\n    24→If my research is correct, we will no longer need to abduct spellcasters in order to create more alhoon! \n    25→Without their pesky hivemind withdrawls, the rest of our brothers may join us in studying the necromancy your husband requires.\n    26→\n    27→Your faithful servant, \n    28→Alhoon Ren\n    29→\n    30→\n    31→"}]},"uuid":"196ca97f-e177-428b-9113-bc9cc016b607","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:01.920Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a60e3ae6-f3b8-4645-8c4d-a9e15ac03d03","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"cad7f510-54f0-4abf-988b-0594c525b55c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DjNm3fU5gWCg7AkPXD5vuJ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: handout\n     3→title: \"Spell Plague Notes 3\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Handouts/Spell Plague Notes 3.txt\"]\n     5→given_to: []\n     6→chapter: \"\"\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→Experiment 626:\n    10→\n    11→The convert is quite responsive today, it seems that a healthy dosage of ground umberhulk eyes and blood from the dark lady does the trick of restoring individial thought to the host.\n    12→Never could I have believe vampirism would be the key to liberating the mindflayer puppets of an elder brain!\n    13→\n    14→It seems that the next generation of ALHOON can now be Ullitharid, with this discovery and the spell plague, it seems that we are on the dawn of a new empire!\n    15→\n    16→However I find myself... curious as to how this plague affects other species. Humanity and its closer cousins seem to have a rather, unintended effects. \n    17→\n    18→After experimenting on my dinner last night. It seems that there are some chosen few amongst the humans who are immune to this spell plague.\n    19→Could it be that some of our own are also immune?\n    20→\n    21→"}]},"uuid":"f2e0c5c4-9f8d-4d8d-b118-5e6956357258","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:02.366Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cad7f510-54f0-4abf-988b-0594c525b55c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"e4beaed7-cc82-4520-a8e7-ed93de443efe","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01GPs6qVt2PvrxwSBecu6bN2","toolUseID":"toolu_01GPs6qVt2PvrxwSBecu6bN2","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:02.695Z","uuid":"51a96b26-f3ad-4dbc-8f6f-30da8aa89409","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"c7823db0-4ce9-496e-b65f-0cbf32c56a63","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GPs6qVt2PvrxwSBecu6bN2","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: location\n     3→category: region\n     4→name: Underdark\n     5→parent: Mardov\n     6→aliases:\n     7→  - The Deep\n     8→  - The Below\n     9→notable_npcs:\n    10→  - \"[[Nocturnus Wayne]]\"\n    11→  - \"[[Oblivion Wayne]]\"\n    12→  - \"[[Peraxis Phyrexia]]\"\n    13→  - \"[[The Corpseforger]]\"\n    14→chapters:\n    15→  - Act 2\n    16→  - Act 3\n    17→source: Places/Underdark/\n    18→maps:\n    19→  - \"[[MARDOV.jpg]]\"\n    20→tags:\n    21→  - region\n    22→  - underdark\n    23→  - drow\n    24→  - mindflayer\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→# Underdark\n    28→\n    29→The vast subterranean realm beneath the mountains of [[Mardov]]. A world of eternal darkness, bioluminescent fungi, underground rivers, and warring factions. The Underdark is divided between Drow-controlled and Mind Flayer-controlled territories, with the party's stronghold ([[D'Guarda Fortress]]) carved out between them.\n    30→\n    31→## Geography & Architecture\n    32→\n    33→### Landscape\n    34→A multi-layered cave system with distinct ecological zones. The upper levels connect to the surface via mountain passages, mine shafts, and the Ways Tunnels. The [[Myconid Grove]] is a bioluminescent fungal forest with towering mushroom canopies and the BloodVine River (a Feywild ley-line). The deeper levels descend into the Kaivan Layer (Drow territory) and the Ulthradi Layer (Mind Flayer territory).\n    35→\n    36→### Architectural Style\n    37→Varied by faction. The Drow city of Kaivan features web-draped spires and temples to Lolth -- the Shrine of the Bat is a beacon-lit temple. The Mind Flayer city of Ulthradi is organic and alien. The [[D'Guarda Fortress]] (after renovation by the party) is a practical military stronghold with a Sesame Tower, teleportation room, and aboleth lair beneath. The [[corpseforge]] contains ancient dwarven ziggurat architecture channeling Moradin.\n    38→\n    39→### Color Palette\n    40→Deep purples, bioluminescent greens and blues, fungal oranges, and the pale grey of raw stone. Lichen-covered doorways glow with ambient light. The Myconid Grove pulses with organic color.\n    41→\n    42→### Lighting & Atmosphere\n    43→No natural light. Everything is lit by bioluminescence, forge-fire, magical emanation, or waystone warmth. The mood ranges from claustrophobic menace in the tunnels to eerie beauty in the fungal caverns. Rot trolls and zombie dwarves lurk in the darker passages.\n    44→\n    45→### Scale & Density\n    46→Massive but sparsely populated. Great distances of empty tunnel between population centers.\n    47→\n    48→## Notable Features\n    49→\n    50→- **The Ways Tunnels** -- Ancient transit network with waystone stations, used by \"Moles\" (underground guides)\n    51→- **Chamber of the Sleepless** -- Lair of an Aboleth, with its Scion and the \"Original Sea Urchin\"\n    52→- **The Corpseforge** -- Ancient dwarven temple channeling Moradin, now corrupted. Contains the ghost of King Korrin Phyrexia.\n    53→- **Vorago Pass** -- Exit point from the Underdark to the surface\n    54→- **The Skaagal Forest** -- Subterranean stone forest\n    55→- **Green Tides of St Ashbury** -- Hidden gnome settlement with smuggler's waterway exit\n    56→\n    57→## Sub-locations\n    58→\n    59→### Fortresses & Cities\n    60→- [[D'Guarda Fortress]] -- Party stronghold (Group A), renovated from ruins\n    61→- [[Da'Gahar]] -- Underground settlement with Phoenix Guard\n    62→- [[kaivan]] -- Drow-controlled layer with streets, temples, and the city of webs (Nerub Gazail)\n    63→- [[ulthradi]] -- Mind Flayer-controlled layer\n    64→\n    65→### Dungeons & Lairs\n    66→- [[Myconid Grove]] -- Fungal forest with the Lair of the Corpseforger\n    67→- [[corpseforge]] -- Ancient dwarven temple with Chamber Channeling Moradin\n    68→- [[Chamber of the Sleepless]] -- Aboleth lair\n    69→\n    70→### Landmarks\n    71→- [[Green Tides of St Ashbury]] -- Hidden gnome settlement with shrine to Avandra\n    72→- [[Shrine of the Bat]] -- Drow temple in the Kaivan Layer, connected to Nocturnus's zodiac\n    73→\n    74→## History\n    75→\n    76→The Underdark's power structure is defined by two competing factions. The **Drow** control the Kaivan Layer, led by [[Oblivion Wayne]] -- Nocturnus's vampire aunt who gave her soul to [[Tiamat]] for an ascension ritual. She threatens an invasion of the surface. The **Mind Flayers** control the Ulthradi Layer, with [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] (an Illithilich) secretly manipulating [[Vag'Mahar]]'s three secret police forces from below.\n    77→\n    78→The party established their stronghold at [[D'Guarda Fortress]] after clearing it of the Aboleth known as \"The Sleepless\" and a sea hag coven (Jesabell, Rea, and Nagani). The Corpseforger -- an entity in the Myconid Grove -- created zombie dwarves from \"The Missing 30\" and threatened the region until confronted.\n    79→\n    80→## Gallery\n    81→\n    82→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Chamber Of the Sleepless.jpg]]\n    83→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Skaagal Forest.jpg]]\n    84→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/The Ways Tunnels.jpg]]\n    85→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Underdark Warming Waystone.jpg]]\n    86→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Vorago Pass -- Exit from the Underdark.jpg]]\n    87→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Dragonslayer's_passage.jpg]]\n    88→"}]},"uuid":"b762cba2-b23b-4e75-8c68-453cfa5c822e","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:02.695Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c7823db0-4ce9-496e-b65f-0cbf32c56a63","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"9586261c-b8e6-43d0-b86b-cd517a83a539","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"af7064736bab05e50","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015KW171P6H3iPHAHyib6SGc","type":"tool_result","content":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-2/Chapter 18 - The Underdark Vision.md-41-\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-2/Chapter 18 - The Underdark Vision.md-42-- The Dueregar army vision foreshadows the War Zenith conflict\nmardov-vault/chapters/act-2/Chapter 18 - The Underdark Vision.md:43:- Tiamat cult activity among the Dueregar connects to Oblivion Wayne's plots\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-2/Chapter 18 - The Underdark Vision.md-44-\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-2/Chapter 18 - The Underdark Vision.md-45-## Slideshow\n--\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-86-Reynaldine is trying to use Tiamat against Asmodeus to save himself AND Gabriel at the stone table. The quest to go and open the winters zenith was ultimately to get her kicked out and force her to use the winter fey entrance, directly to him. He also knew the last seal of Asmodeus was there so they could use that to give Gratz the power to ascend and stand a chance against the goddess\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-87-\nmardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md:88:What he didn't plan on is them forging a new pillar of Harmony within Bahamuts domain which warrants an another arch angel, Nocturnus Wayne, Archangel of Harmony, Ender of Oblivion and Fated to kill Ashara Everstar (Tiamats host)\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-89-\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-90-At the same time, he didn't anticipate that Rumblesin would pick Josh as his knight and thought it would be Sprocket, Josh being too pure of heart is going to be a problem for him as well.\n--\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-98-Tiamat is getting them to the stone table by fighting Kelik over the Convergence and instead of using Lapis like she wanted, he will end up putting her right at the stone table as that's halfway between them.\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-99-\nmardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md:100:Vecna is the one who killed the Amethyst Dragon and the sphinx. He's going to use that to tip the battle in Tiamats favor. His goal is to get her to kill Bahamut AND Oblivion. He wants to pick up the pieces of both of these deities because they are tied to the sealing technique used in the Zeniths. A fate he fears that the Raven Queen is saving for him.\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md-101-\n--\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/locations/regions/underdark.md-74-## History\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/locations/regions/underdark.md-75-\nmardov-vault/locations/regions/underdark.md:76:The Underdark's power structure is defined by two competing factions.
