Here are the two complete timelines:\n\n---\n\n# EUCLID -- Complete Timeline\n\n## Ancient Era (Pre-Campaign)\n\n1. **Creation of the Celestial Realm** -- Euclid begins as a fledgling Owl Aarakocra archivist working under Gabriel the Kind, one of the three Archangels (alongside Barachiel the Just and Dominel the Righteous) who ruled the 7 Heavens. [CANON] (Source: `mardov-vault/lore/Euclid.md`)\n\n2. **Promotion to Keeper of the Divine Seal** -- Euclid works his way up to become Gabriel's Keeper of the Divine Seal, one of the 13 Seals of Asmodeus. The condition: Gabriel would teach him a Forbiddence Spell to keep him safe from Fiends. [CANON] (Source: `mardov-vault/lore/Euclid.md`)\n\n3. **Mentoring Reynardine** -- A young Reynardine of the Autumn Court approaches Euclid to study magic. They spend countless years researching the Progenitors together. A key text is \"The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors.\" Reynardine recovers artifacts for Euclid, including donating his own pan-flute. In exchange, Reynardine promises Euclid \"a favor and the moon.\" [CANON] (Source: `mardov-vault/lore/Euclid.md`)\n\n4. **Alexandria Created** -- Euclid's library on the moon is named Alexandria (also called \"Euclid's Study\" and \"Hall of Living Memory\"). It is tethered to Laputa Town, the Aarakocra ancestral home on an isle of clouds. [CANON] (Sources: `lore/Euclid.md`, `lore/Alexandria.md`)\n\n5. **Grazzt Steals the Divine Seal** -- The Demon Prince Grazzt tricks Juliannia of the Summer Court into falling in love with him, then steals the Divine Seal from Euclid. Gabriel sacrifices himself to contain Grazzt, and both are imprisoned in the broken seal. Reynardine, holding the shattered seal and Juliannia's demonic amulet, vows to \"fix everything\" -- creating the abomination Reynaldine of the Autumn Court. [CANON] (Source: `mardov-vault/lore/Gabriel the Kind.md`)\n\n6. **Fall of the 7 Heavens** -- Reynaldine takes Gabriel's Divine Seal, causing the collapse of the Celestial Realm and the 9 Hells (they merge into the Feywilde). Euclid retreats to Alexandria on the moon, entrusting Archimedes (the Owl Zodiac) to transfer the Forbiddence Spell to their new refuge. [CANON] (Sources: `lore/Euclid.md`, `lore-extraction/Chapter 38X - Nyx s notes - Euclid s Study.md`)\n\n7. **The Elemental Wars & Treaty** -- During the Elemental Wars, Euclid makes an agreement with the Air Genasi forces (followers of the fallen Reynardine). He allows Laputa Town to be anchored to the newly formed Cloud City, modifying the Forbiddence Spell into a contract only undoable by the Ragnarok Protocol. The new spell bars Reynaldine from Cloud City. A peace treaty is formed as a coalition between three parties: **Terrell Temptus (the Storm King)**, **Euclid**, and **Leonidas of Summer's Last Light**. Terms: Fire Genasi hand over the Water Genasi princess; Windhaven's Harvest Festival created as tithe to the Spring Court; Leonidas granted a sanctum preserving Juliannia's last moments forever. [CANON] (Sources: `lore/Euclid.md`, `lore/Terrell Temptus, The Storm King.md`)\n\n8. **Euclid establishes his office at The Roost** -- Euclid's Study is accessible through The Roost Hotel and Casino in Cloud City, beneath the Guest Services area. The Owl Zodiac Archimedes guards the space, visible as a blinking yellow-white presence through the window. [CANON] (Sources: `dm-notes/Cloud City Arc.md`, `player-notes/ch048T-brigit.md`)\n\n## Campaign Era\n\n9. **Chapter 38X -- Nyx and Stalwart visit Euclid's Study** -- Euclid pulls Nyx and Stalwart through a portal to his library (he only wanted one of them). He reveals: he was Keeper of the Divine Seal but \"is no longer\"; Reynaldine is his enemy; the 7 Heavens fell; the Origins of Genasi book is missing (Reynardine stole it). He identifies that Nyx's pact may be with Juliannia, and Xyn's with Gabriel. Euclid believes it possible to bring Juliannia and Gabriel back -- but needs Xyn, Tygras, and Reynardine's pactbound. He warns Nyx she is \"on thin ice\" as a Tome-Sworn connected to Reynaldine. [CANON] (Source: `lore-extraction/Chapter 38X - Nyx s notes - Euclid s Study.md`)\n\n10. **Chapters 48-49T -- Cloud City Arc / The Roost** -- The party arrives at Cloud City and The Roost. Mr. Tux (penguin Aarakocra hotel manager) reports fiends are assaulting Euclid's Study in Guest Services. Euclid \"hasn't left his study in quite some time\" but Mr. Tux spoke to him recently and he seemed fine. The fiend problem has lasted 3-6 weeks. The party must clear the fiends before reaching Euclid. [CANON] (Source: `lore-extraction/Chapter 48T Brigit s Notes The Roost Hotel and Casino teamBattle.md`)\n\n11. **Chapter 55T -- Return to Cloud City** -- The party plans to head to the Roost to \"get it back online\" and save Euclid and Archimedes. Nyx examines the Origin of Genasi book (Xyn had the only copy), finding Xyn's handwriting. It contains a ritual connected to the Progenitors and a prophecy about the Harvest Zenith. [CANON] (Source: `player-notes/ch055T-brigit.md`)\n\n12. **Euclid's book as the key to Harvest's Zenith** -- Xyn tells Nyx that the key to opening the Harvest's Zenith is written within \"The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors,\" the book originally stolen from Euclid by Reynaldine. [CANON] (Source: `dm-notes/Cloud City Arc.md`)\n\n13. **Chapter 107 -- Ragnarok** -- The Ragnarok Protocol is activated, removing the Forbiddence Spell from Alexandria, Cloud City, and WindHaven. This leaves Euclid's sanctuaries unprotected from fiends and Reynaldine. [CANON] (Source: `lore/Euclid.md`)\n\n14. **Post-Ragnarok -- Forbiddence Spell Reactivated** -- Stalwart, Nyx, and Brigit (using her Owl Zodiac Form) re-activate the Forbiddence Spell. However, Xyn and her patron Mr. Happy (Gabriel) stay behind to finish charging the spell. [CANON] (Source: `lore/Euclid.md`)\n\n15. **Harvest's Zenith connection** -- Gabriel's power is split three ways: majority trapped in Reynaldine, a portion locked in the Harvest's Zenith (behind a door requiring four Elemental Evil seals), and a fragment in Mr. Happy (Xyn's teddy bear). The Harvest's Zenith is the Zenith of Famine, and Euclid's scholarly work is the key to accessing it. [CANON] (Source: `lore/Gabriel the Kind.md`)\n\n### Off-Screen Status [INFERRED]\n- After Ragnarok, Euclid's fate in Alexandria is unclear -- the Forbiddence Spell was reactivated, but Xyn remained behind charging it, suggesting a vulnerable gap.\n- Euclid likely remains at Alexandria or The Roost, continuing as keeper of ancient knowledge. His role is as scholar and guardian, not combatant.\n\n---\n\n# OBLIVION WAYNE -- Complete Timeline\n\n## Pre-Campaign Era\n\n1. **Oblivion deposes her brother, unites all Drow** -- Oblivion Wayne, a Drow noblewoman and Nocturnus Wayne's aunt, uses alpha vampire power to depose her own brother (Nocturnus's father) and unite all Drow under vampire rule. \"The Drow are no more. Long live the Vampire Queen!\" [CANON] (Sources: `lore/timeline.md`, `lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md`, `characters/npcs/major/Oblivion Wayne.md`)\n\n2. **Marriage to Ebon Wayne** -- Oblivion marries Ebon Wayne, a vampire Drow. [CANON] (Source: `characters/npcs/major/Oblivion Wayne.md`)\n\n3. **Ebon Wayne captures the Bat Zodiac** -- Ebon captures the Bat Zodiac spirit and uses the \"gift of the first bite\" to create alpha vampires. Each turned vampire has innate loyalty to Oblivion. This is the mechanism by which she builds her vampire army of converted Drow. [CANON] (Sources: `lore/timeline.md`, `lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md`, `factions/vecna-sworn.md`)\n\n4. **Ebon Wayne discovers the Spell Plague** -- Through the captured Bat Zodiac and Underdark research, Ebon Wayne discovers the Spell Plague -- a disease of unknown (possibly extraplanar) origin that affects humanoids' faith and connection to the divine. [CANON] (Sources: `lore/timeline.md`, `sources/handouts/Spell Plague Notes 1.md`)\n\n5. **Alliance with Alhoon Ren** -- Alhoon Ren, a Mind Flayer researcher faithful to Oblivion, works on combining Umber Hulk essence with Oblivion's vampiric blood. This liberates Mind Flayers from Elder Brain control and creates a new generation of Ullitharid Alhoon. Ren writes letters to \"My Dark Lady Wayne\" detailing experiments. [CANON] (Sources: `handouts/Spell Plague Notes 1-3.md`)\n\n6. **Nocturnus turned against his will** -- Oblivion turns her nephew Nocturnus into a vampire against his will. He carries the vampirism as both curse and weapon, seeking vengeance. [CANON] (Source: `characters/pcs/Nocturnus Wayne.md`)\n\n7. **Oblivion gives her soul to Tiamat** -- Oblivion Wayne gives her soul to Tiamat via a bat-worship ascension ritual, ascending beyond normal vampire queen status. [CANON] (Sources: `locations/regions/underdark.md`, `lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md`)\n\n8. **Obsession with the ancient Warforged army** -- Oblivion becomes obsessed with the ancient Warforged army (originally built by Thorin Bronzebeard), believing warforged are key to dominating the Illithid and conquering the surface. [CANON] (Sources: `characters/npcs/major/Oblivion Wayne.md`, `lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md`)\n\n## Campaign Era -- Off-Screen Actions\n\n9. **Acts 1-2 (Chapters 0-40) -- Building power in the Underdark** -- Oblivion controls the Kaivan Layer of the Underdark. Her vampire-converted Drow army grows. Alhoon Ren continues Spell Plague and vampirism research. Ebon Wayne's vassal works with Peraxis Phyrexia. Nocturnus is spotted in the area, and Ren deploys gnome patrols to \"squash this last pathetic remnant of his father's rule.\" [CANON] (Sources: `handouts/Spell Plague Notes 2.md`, `factions/vecna-sworn.md`)\n\n10. **Chapter 18 -- Underdark Vision** -- The party encounters Dueregar worshipping Tiamat. DM notes confirm this cult activity \"connects to Oblivion Wayne's plots.\" [CANON] (Source: `chapters/act-2/Chapter 18 - The Underdark Vision.md`)\n\n11. **Chapter 34 -- Nocturnus kills cousin Dusken** -- Nocturnus Wayne kills his cousin Dusken and becomes the \"Drider King,\" further escalating the Wayne family conflict. [CANON] (Source: `lore/timeline.md`)\n\n12. **Acts 2-3 -- Vecna Sworn operations** -- As an Allied Agent under the Zenith of War, Oblivion serves Vecna's agenda targeting the War's Zenith / Mountain's Zenith. She works alongside Peraxis Phyrexia (the primary agent) and Ghost-Eye Thorrin. Her vampire army and warforged obsession position her as one of the campaign's most dangerous antagonists. She plans a Drow/Illithid vampire invasion of the overlands. [CANON] (Sources: `factions/vecna-sworn.md`, `characters/npcs/major/Oblivion Wayne.md`)\n\n13. **Alhoon Ren's breakthrough (Experiment 626)** -- Ren achieves his goal: ground Umber Hulk eyes combined with Oblivion's blood restores individual thought to Mind Flayer hosts. \"Never could I have believed vampirism would be the key to liberating the mindflayer puppets of an elder brain!\" This creates a new Ullitharid generation and \"the dawn of a new empire.\" [CANON] (Source: `handouts/Spell Plague Notes 3.md`)\n\n14. **Chapter 107 -- Ragnarok / Warforged Awakening** -- Ragnarok occurs. Warforged activate. All artificially created beings (Warforged, Eladrin, Aasimar, Drab, Myconid, Kenku) are commanded to turn on the Gods. This directly relates to Oblivion's warforged obsession -- the army she coveted is now activated for a different purpose. [INFERRED] (Source: `chapters/act-5/chapter-107.md`)\n\n15. **Chapter 108 -- Lolth's revelation** -- Casaval reveals three beings: Irethia (prototype), Fen (warden), and Nocturnus (bane). The Drow \"truly belong to Lolth\" not the Raven Queen. Lolth's \"Two Truths and a Lie\" reshapes the Drow power dynamic that Oblivion relies on. [CANON] (Source: `chapters/act-5/chapter-108.md`, `lore/timeline.md`)\n\n16. **Chapter 109 -- Nocturnus cured of vampirism** -- Josh Coolbreeze cleanses Nocturnus of vampirism. This severs the vampiric bond tying Nocturnus to Oblivion's bloodline. The Lolth-Raven Queen deal is also revealed. [CANON] (Sources: `lore/timeline.md`, `lore-extraction/character-timelines.md`)\n\n17. **Chapter 131 -- DM notes reveal cosmic stakes** -- Vecna wants to kill both Tiamat AND Oblivion, picking up pieces of both deities because \"they are tied to the sealing technique used in the Zeniths.\" Nocturnus is identified as \"Archangel of Harmony, Ender of Oblivion and Fated to kill Ashara Everstar (Tiamat's host).\" [CANON] (Source: `chapters/act-5/chapter-131.md`)\n\n18. **Nocturnus's Prophecy -- The destined confrontation** -- The Nocturnus Prophecy lays out four steps: (1) Travel to Vag Mahar, (2) Enter the Mountain's Zenith, (3) Find the Kiln of the First Flame and Light the First Forge (this will provoke an invasion by Oblivion Wayne), (4) Seek out aunt in childhood home -- \"only then can you truly kill her.\" [CANON] (Source: `handouts/Nocturnus Prophecy.md`)\n\n19. **Chapters 135-136 -- Nocturnus ascends** -- Nocturnus dies fighting Tiamat, is revived (fulfilling Shattar Kai destiny), absorbs a fragment of Asmodeus's essence, and fully ascends. He kills Tiamat. Promise emerges: \"My liege, I swear to you. You've fulfilled the darkest night prophecy.\" Nocturnus must return to the Underdark to rule. [CANON] (Source: `lore-extraction/character-timelines.md`)\n\n20. **Chapter 136 -- Vecna gains knowledge of Oblivion** -- Upon Tiamat's death, Vecna gains knowledge from both fallen gods, including \"how to bring about Oblivion and to Subjugate even the most powerful of beings.\" Nocturnus must restart the Drow population. [CANON] (Source: `chapters/act-5/chapter-136.md`)\n\n## Oblivion's Ascended Stat Block\n- AC 17, HP 400, 20th-level cleric spellcasting + Matron Mother abilities + full Vampire abilities\n- Lolth's Fickle Favor, Magic Resistance, Shapechanger, Misty Escape, Regeneration\n- Multiattack: Demon Staff (psychic + frighten), Tentacle Rod, or Bite\n- 3 Legendary Actions per round\n- This is the form Nocturnus must ultimately face. [CANON] (Source: `characters/creatures/Oblivion Wayne Ascended.md`)\n\n## What She Was Doing During Each Act [SPECULATIVE/INFERRED]\n\n| Act | Oblivion's Off-Screen Activity |\n|-----|-------------------------------|\n| **Act 1 (Ch 0-13)** | [INFERRED] Consolidating Drow under vampire rule. Alhoon Ren researching Spell Plague. Ebon Wayne experimenting with Bat Zodiac. Building vampire army. |\n| **Act 2 (Ch 14-40)** | [CANON] Ren writes to her about Spell Plague experiments and Experiment 626. Nocturnus spotted in her territory. Dueregar Tiamat cult active. Peraxis collaborating with Ebon's vassals. |\n| **Act 3 (Ch 41-86)** | [INFERRED] Expanding warforged obsession. Maintaining alliance with Peraxis and Ghost-Eye Thorrin. Planning overland invasion. Ren achieves the vampirism/Umber Hulk breakthrough. |\n| **Act 4 (Ch 87-107)** | [INFERRED] Warforged awakening at Ragnarok disrupts her plans -- the army she wanted now serves a different master. Lolth's claim on the Drow threatens her authority structure. |\n| **Act 5 (Ch 108-136)** | [CANON/INFERRED] Vecna maneuvers to destroy both Tiamat and Oblivion. Nocturnus ascends as \"Ender of Oblivion.\" Nocturnus fulfills the Darkest Night prophecy but the Nocturnus Prophecy (killing Oblivion in her childhood home) appears to be still ahead. |\n\n---\n\n## Key Relationship Summary\n\n**Euclid <-> Oblivion Wayne**: No direct connection in the sources. They operate in entirely different spheres -- Euclid in the celestial/arcane/scholarly domain (Cloud City, Alexandria), Oblivion in the Underdark/war/vampiric domain.
